## Module Map (non-obvious wiring)

- `src/main.rs` is the entire CLI: `format`, `status`, `clients`, `claim`, `mount`, `umount`, `bench`, `clone`, `tune`, `config`, `volume` (PR VL3: `add-data`/`list` — durable `vol-` identity, KD-5; VL4/VL5b added drain/remove + meta add/remove/migrate), `fsck`/`scrub` (PR VL6a/VL6b), `defrag` (PR VL7 — §5.7 four-axis model, `--report-only`/`--data`/`--meta`/`--fold`/`--rebalance`, live via the admin lane or offline via the D0-guarded coordinator), `job`, `df`, `storage`, `nvmeof`. `src/lib.rs` is the library surface (incl. `DataVolumeRecord` + `FormatConfig::resolved_data_volumes` legacy grandfathering). (The former `config` fake admin verbs were deleted in the volume-lifecycle program's PR VL1 — `docs/design-volume-lifecycle.md` — and refuse loudly naming their successors.)
- `src/fuse_client.rs` — FUSE daemon + `format_volume_ext` / `SqueezefsFilesystem`.
- `src/defrag.rs` — the PR VL7 online-defrag measurement engine (KD-11 four-axis model: D1 free-space contiguity, D2 file locality, D3 staged-extent pressure, D4 meta node occupancy; `frag_*` gauges + `--report-only`; the §5.8 offline report probe and D0-guarded offline mover harness). Movers live on the job fabric (`src/jobs.rs` `JobType::Defrag{Data,Meta,Fold}`) and REUSE existing machinery: the VL4 `move_one` engine with contiguity-aware destination picks, the W2 fold, the KV SMO compactor.
- `src/fsck.rs` — the PR VL6a/VL6b online fsck: detection classes C1–C7 (incl. the C7 data scrub), the verify-before-report zero-FP ladder (suspect/settle/lease-re-check; allocation-epoch + in-flight-registry + mover-ledger exemptions for C2/C3), offline `--shards k/N` + `merge-reports`, and the §5.6a per-class repair planner/executor (dry-run default, quarantine-first). `src/job_wire.rs` — the §5.1.6 remote job-shard execution wire (enrollment, leases, fencing-checked result proposals, WERO preemption ladder).
- `src/routing.rs` — `DataRouter` (progressive layout: inline / staged / striped) plus the `OnceCell`-held `CryptoCompressState`.
- `src/dlm.rs` — distributed lock manager (`DlmClient`, `acquire_lock`, fencing tokens, heartbeat renewal).
- `src/env_knobs.rs` — the env-knob REGISTRY (name, kind, admissible range, default, purpose for every `SQUEEZEFS_*`/`SQZ_*` variable) plus the startup refusal gate `main` calls before anything is parsed or mounted, and the `bool_knob`/`int_knob`/`opt_int_knob`/`enum_knob` readers every site uses. The value law itself is `crates/squeezefs-ipc/src/env_knob_core.rs`, `#[path]`-shared into the root crate, the fuse3 fork and the preload shim (ENG-10).
- `src/block_allocator.rs`, `src/nvme_dev.rs`, `src/storage.rs` — block allocation + NVMe/LVM pool plumbing.
- `src/block_reclaim.rs` — the background block-reclaim queue (2026-07-27 overwrite-throughput fix): terminal frees enqueue their device reclaim (`BLKDISCARD`/`PUNCH_HOLE`) + `finish_free` here instead of issuing them on the write path; exactly-once by pop ownership, offsets stay non-reallocatable until reclaimed, ENOSPC pressure valve wired into `BlockAllocator::allocate_block`, unmount teardown drains, and the queue observes the D0 writer-guard fence (the per-volume `failed` latch, once per batch): a fenced holder ceases ALL device reclaims permanently — entries drop without `finish_free` (successor recovery owns the accounting) into `block_free_reclaim_fence_halts`. Since the 2026-07-31 write-wall campaign + iteration loop (`.benchmarks/2026-07-31-write-wall.md` — the field's rewrite wall: queue pinned at cap with most reclaims running inline on the write path; the width experiments proved the TARGET deallocate service is the under-load drain ceiling at ANY client width): the worker drains across **demand-derived parallel blocking-pool lanes** (group by device → sort → consecutive-slice chunking so adjacency survives into each lane's coalescer; one lane per `batch_blocks` of a device's queued demand, capped per device; pop-ownership/`processing`-reservation/finish_free-after-reclaim/fence-per-lane invariants unchanged), under the **reclaim manners law** — foreground DEVICE-byte movement + queue below cap ⇒ the drain DEFERS entirely (a stats poller can never hold it deferred — the probe is device-plane bytes, not ops); at-cap ⇒ drain regardless (parked enqueues are foreground writers); idle ⇒ full-width catch-up — and **park-don't-spill**: an at-cap enqueue parks (async, bounded by `SQUEEZEFS_RECLAIM_CAP_PARK_MS`, default 1000) and soft-overflows on expiry; device commands never issue from the enqueue context (the retired inline arm charged a field-measured 12–22 ms synchronous fabric round-trip to the write path). **Since W-4 (e2e perf audit ladder row 14, 2026-09-05, `.benchmarks/2026-09-05-w4-reclaim-derivation.md` — mechanism landed, field rows owed) the park is EVENT-DRIVEN and both liveness constants DERIVE**: the cap's population is queued + in-flight (the count `queue_bytes` always gauged), `finish_free` runs per coalesced range and wakes parked producers (room flows at the drain rate, never in pass-sized bursts), and the quantum is only the stalled-drain safety bound — `bound = clamp(4 × batch_blocks ÷ measured drain rate, 50 ms, 1000 ms)` (the shipped 1 s, which the 2026-09-01 rewrite rows read straight off the p99.9, is now the CEILING; fleet under load ≈ 140 ms), `cap = clamp(measured displacement rate × that room latency, 4096 = the shipped floor, budget/1024 ÷ entry RAM)`. Knobs `SQUEEZEFS_RECLAIM_BATCH_{BLOCKS,MS}` / `SQUEEZEFS_RECLAIM_LANES_PER_DEV` (default 32, idle-engaged) / `SQUEEZEFS_RECLAIM_QUEUE_MAX_BLOCKS` + `SQUEEZEFS_RECLAIM_CAP_PARK_MS` (explicit overrides of the derived defaults — `4096` / `1000` are the A/B levers); counters `block_free_reclaim_{queued,queue_bytes,batches,sync_drains,fence_halts,commands,cap_parks,cap_overflow}` + the derivation faces `block_free_reclaim_{drain_rate,arrival_rate,queue_cap,park_bound_ms,park_tick_wakes}`. The sibling write-path lever: `SQUEEZEFS_INPLACE_OVERWRITE=1` opts a substrate into IN-PLACE eligible full-block overwrites (`write_through_inplace_overwrites`; zero displacement/dealloc — the W1 whole-block face; **default OFF, substrate-measured**: on zram-lz4 targets a slot-replace write costs ≈ 2× a fresh-slot write, so CoW + deferred discard + manners wins there; real-SSD DSM fleets opt in).
- `src/cache/`, `src/tiering/` — tiered cache (GDS / RAM LRU / NVMe staging) and tier selection.
- `src/crypto_compress.rs` — compression (lz4/zstd) + symmetric AEAD encryption, applied across all three write paths via `process_write` / `process_read`. **The key wrap is HKDF-SHA-256 → the volume's own AEAD, both from `ring`** (`src/keyfile.rs`, scheme `hkdf-sha256/aead-kw/v2`; design `docs/design-key-handling.md`): KW-1 / ruling D3 REPLACED the RSA primitive rather than adjudicate `rsa 0.9`'s RUSTSEC-2023-0071 (Marvin) — do not reintroduce `rsa`. Key material resolves from `--encrypt-key`, `SQUEEZEFS_ENCRYPT_KEY_FILE`, or `/etc/squeezefs/keys/<key_id>.key`.
- `src/jobs.rs`, `src/nvmeof/`, `src/config_ops.rs` — maintenance-job worker skeleton (throttle law + queue; the volume-lifecycle fabric extends it), NVMe-oF control, and the offline **guarded** admin verbs over durable on-volume state (cache-path policy, `volume add-data`/state — PR VL3). (The former `src/p2p.rs`/`src/tiering/dht.rs` peer subsystem was unreachable and deleted, ENG-13; its shared cluster TLS core lives on as `src/tiering/cluster_tls.rs` for the job wire.) (Staging crash recovery lives in `src/cache/nvme.rs` — `NvmeStaging::new`; the former `src/recovery.rs` stub was deleted, ENG-12.) The historical `/dev/shm` runtime-config file is DELETED (VL3): live `enable`/`disable` health overrides ride the admin lane (`BackendRouter::set_health_override`); offline enable/disable is durable `DataVolumeRecord.state`.

## Repo-specific conventions

- **Cache-path policy (mount can never override):** staging/cache directories (`--disk-cache-paths`) are declared at **format** and recorded in the format config — the single source of truth. `mount` reads them from the config and **rejects** the flag with a loud error; format without the flag ⇒ a permanently **cache-less** filesystem (RAM tiers + direct block I/O; beyond-inline writes route striped — no staged layout, no conjured default staging dir). Changing paths is the admin op `squeezefs config set-cache-paths <sqmeta-uri> <paths...>` (format-grade live-client refusal; wipes the new dirs so generation stamping starts clean) with `get-cache-paths` for reads. Contracts pinned in `tests/cache_path_policy_tests.rs`.
- **Env-knob convention (ONE law, ENG-10 — `src/env_knobs.rs` is the registry, `crates/squeezefs-ipc/src/env_knob_core.rs` the shared parser `#[path]`-included by the root crate, the fuse3 fork and the shim):** absent = unset/empty/whitespace-only; booleans are `1/true/yes/on` vs `0/false/no/off` (case-insensitive — `=0` DISABLES, including knobs whose default is ON; the retired presence-based form made `SQUEEZEFS_FREE_FORENSICS=0` *enable* forensics); integers carry an admissible range and out-of-range is a **refusal, not a clamp**; explicit > percentage > derived precedence unchanged. A malformed/out-of-range/retired knob **refuses the process at startup**, naming every offender, before anything is opened or mounted (`env_knobs::refusal_report()` in `main`) — so no call site may `panic!` on a knob value (a panic in a mount-time constructor is `abort` under the release profile) and none may silently default one. An unregistered `SQUEEZEFS_*`/`SQZ_*` name is announced as a probable typo, never refused. **Adding a knob means adding a registry entry** — `tests/env_knob_convention_tests.rs` fails the gate on an unregistered knob literal, which is what keeps the knob surface documented. The LD_PRELOAD shim is the one documented asymmetry: it announces a bad value and keeps the default rather than killing its host application. Operator surface: `docs/operations.md` §Environment knobs.
- **Staging/cache file modes (VAL-7b, 2026-08-05):** every staging and read-cache **directory** is `0700` and every **segment ring file** `0600` — on a passthrough volume those bytes are literal plaintext user data, and the segment headers carry staged object keys regardless. `config_ops::create_private_dir_all` is the ONE policy point (`STAGING_DIR_MODE` / `STAGING_FILE_MODE`); it deliberately does **not** use `O_NOFOLLOW`, because the mount layout's `<isolated>/cache_segment → ../cache_segment` symlink (the shared read cache) is load-bearing and `O_DIRECTORY|O_NOFOLLOW` on a symlink is `ENOTDIR`. `O_NOFOLLOW` belongs to the format-grade stamp (`stamp_precleared_staging_dir`), which applies mode AND ownership through one `O_DIRECTORY|O_NOFOLLOW` fd (`fchmod` + `fchown`) — the path-based `chown` it replaced followed a symlink planted between the create and the chown, as root, on an operator-supplied path.
- **`--log-file` is `0600` + `O_NOFOLLOW`** (VAL-7h, `squeezefs::open_log_file` — all three open sites): the log carries device paths, staging dirs and key names; a symlinked target refuses `ELOOP` and, per ENG-3, fails the mount rather than redirecting. **Root subprocess `PATH` is sanitized once at startup** (VAL-7i, `harden_root_subprocess_path` — drops empty/relative/non-dir/group-or-world-writable entries, falls back to the system path, never empty): argv-only was already the discipline, this closes the *resolution* half for the ~63 root subprocess sites.
- **The ADMIN lane runs the data plane's ladder** (VAL-7c): ABI + `build_commit` equality → bootstrap-nonce freshness → `SO_PEERCRED`, in that order, mirroring `handle_hello_msg` so the two cannot drift (`AdminHello` carries the three fields; `IPC_ABI` 3). The administering identity is explicit — `--admin-uid` / `-o admin_uid=N` (`admin_uid_from_options`) — instead of inherited from `SUDO_UID`; an unparseable value keeps the fallback and never widens to root.
- Metadata keys are namespaced via the `fs_key!("suffix")` macro and the global `FS_PREFIX` (`src/lib.rs`). Code that touches metadata keys must go through the macro, not hardcode prefixes.
- `WRITE_VERIFICATION` is a process-global `AtomicBool` toggled by `--write-verification` on mount; read-after-write checksum verification uses it. Library code should call `write_verification_enabled()` rather than reading CLI args.
- The architecture uses the **NVMe / NVMe-oF block** backend as the sole primary data path.

## Stats surface

**Access + census posture (VAL-7a, 2026-08-05):** the `.stats`/`.config` inodes are **mode 0400 owned by the mount uid** (`fuse_client::VIRTUAL_INODE_MODE`; `-o default_permissions` is what enforces it) — the payload maps every backing-device path, staging directory, cached block key and per-inode write custody, so it was never safe world-readable on an `allow_other` mount. The **key census** (`read_lru_keys`, `write_lru_keys`, `nvme_staged_write_file_ids`, `nvme_read_cache_block_keys`, `active_writes`) is **opt-in** behind `SQUEEZEFS_STATS_KEY_CENSUS=1` (read live, self-reported as `stats_key_census`); the census-free count gauges always export and are what tooling keys on — `read_lru_key_count`, `write_lru_key_count`, `nvme_staged_write_file_count`, `nvme_read_cache_block_count`, `active_write_block_count` (the last two are `squeezefs umount`'s unflushed-staged-write check). Multi-user resource posture is **single-tenant by declaration** (VAL-7d): no per-uid quota/rate-limit/accounting in the FUSE path, every R5 component process-global — stated in `docs/operations.md` §Multi-user mounts.

Mounted volumes expose process metrics under the virtual **stats** inode (JSON), including layout mix, bg admission, uring queue-full, and lease acquire outcomes. The 2026-08 frontier/rewrite wave adds: the **zcrx lane** family (`zcrx_fill_bytes`, `zcrx_gather_bytes`, `zcrx_lane_poisoned` must-stay-0, area/violation gauges — engagement law `gather ≡ fill`, closure byte-exact; `docs/design-zcrx-read-lane.md`), the **fuse3 transport-geometry/zc** gauges (`transport_max_write`/`transport_max_pages` — the negotiated-geometry law that replaced the hardcoded 256-page plan; `fuse3_kmbuf_negotiated` 0/1, `fuse3_zc_replies`, and the `commit_flush` fifth phase of `read/write_transport_phase_ns`), and the **rewrite program P0** families (`rewrite_{blocks,user_bytes,device_write_bytes}` — the vehicle-blind `rewrite_amp` SLO attribution, gates ≤1.05 + zero mid-row discards; `rewrite_shadow_{swaps,bytes,fallbacks,fence_drops,open_epochs}` with `fence_drops` must-stay-0 and `open_epochs`→0 at quiesce; the discard-elision debt ledger whose law is `queued + elided ≡ terminal frees`; supersession engagement counters — `docs/design-rewrite-program.md`). Prefer these for live regression signals over ad-hoc logging. Per-volume metadata format + durability fields (`meta_format_version`, `meta_volume_atomicity[_physical]`) and the `meta_kv_*` family are listed under **Metadata format: v3 CoW KV (the only format)**; the metadata-throughput program's families (`writer_guard_*`, `meta_commit_group_*` / `meta_conveyor_*`, `meta_kv_fold_*`, `meta_kv_times_echo_*`, `fuse_op_phase_ns` / `fuse_create_under_lock_ns` / `fuse_op_watchdog_overdue`, `transport_commit_batch*`, `writeback_{superseded_noops,stale_token_retries,orphan_discards}`) are listed under **Metadata-throughput program (Implemented 2026-07)**; the L4 interception program (2026-07-19, `docs/design-preload-interception.md` §8, closing `.benchmarks/2026-07-19-l4-interception-closing.md`) adds the session-host families `ipc_sessions_{active,total,poisoned,reaped}`, `ipc_binds[_dev_override]`, `ipc_bind_refused_{version,nonce,flags,mode,budget,peercred,disabled}` (`disabled` = control-plane-only mounts refusing data HELLOs — the expected default-mount posture, counted apart so the security classes stay clean; Rev 8), `ipc_admission_refusals`, `ipc_arena_bytes`, `ipc_descriptor_rejects` (with `ipc_descriptor_rejects`/`ipc_sessions_poisoned` as must-stay-0 tripwires), the data-plane families `ipc_ops_{read,write}` / `ipc_bytes_{in,out}` (**the charter-rule-4 engagement instrument** — an il benchmark row is INVALID unless their deltas account for the row's ops), `ipc_fast_path_serves` vs `ipc_async_handoffs` + the `ipc_fast_path_{lock,miss}_demotions` split (fast-path health), `ipc_service_threads`, the W1 invalidation pair `ipc_inval_{notifies,suppressed}`, and the il hold-probe engagement pair `ipc_hold_probe_{serves,misses}` (2026-08-03 campaign, `.benchmarks/2026-08-03-il-hold-probe.md`: the read-lane hold is the §5.5.1 sync fast path's fourth leg — staging → hot → hold → NVMe read-cache, the handler ladder's order — probed lock-free on the service thread before any demotion/direct-drive; ledger-visible serves dispatch their R1b ceremony to the fuse3 handler lanes exactly-once; a bracket row is INVALID unless the serves delta accounts for its hold serves; 0 by construction under `SQUEEZEFS_READ_LANE=0` and on `direct_device_true` mounts); the shim-parity campaign (2026-07-28, `.benchmarks/2026-07-28-shim-parity.md`, design Rev 14) adds the placed-sever family `placed_{severs,adoptions,merge_elides,sever_fallbacks,assemblies,assembly_bytes}` — the 1-copy ring write path (whole-block ring chunks sever directly into a shared per-(ino, block) assembly that the first merge ADOPTS as the `ActiveBlockBuf` backing; `merge_elides` counts copies avoided, `sever_fallbacks` counts ineligible shapes riding the pooled path, `placed_assembly_bytes` is the non-sheddable `placed_assemblies` R5 component; the write_matrix **il-vs-kernel parity verdict** is the governing instrument: kernel and IPC at minimum at par, IPC ahead in the majority — any beyond-band il loss exits nonzero); the near-zero-copy campaign (2026-07-31, `.benchmarks/2026-07-31-near-zero-copy.md`) adds `nt_copy_bytes` (NT-store engagement at the two DMA-destined copy sites — a census/lever row is INVALID unless its delta accounts for the row's merge/sever bytes; 0 on `SQUEEZEFS_NT_COPY=0` mounts and sub-256 KiB shapes) and the live arena-THP engagement instrument is the daemon's `smaps_rollup` `ShmemPmdMapped` sampled DURING a row (sessions reap at client exit — `tests/copy_census_rig.sh` is the packaged census instrument); the NUMA-affinity campaign (2026-07-31, `perf/numa-affinity`, `.benchmarks/2026-07-31-numa-affinity.md` — measured field verdict: baseline crossing ≈ 50 % proven on every high-volume pass, stage-1 placement takes il rows to 1.000 local, **wr-il sustained +23.7 % / rd-il +12.7 %**, kern rows par, A0 ≈ B pins attribution) adds the **UPI-crossing estimate instrument** `numa_{local,remote}_bytes` (daemon copy passes: the §5.5.2 ring-write sever + the §5.5.1 arena completion serves) and `fuse3_numa_{local,remote}_bytes` (transport passes: FUSE_WRITE lease delivery ≈ the K1 kernel copy, reply-body serves) — classification is DISTANCE-based (`numa_core::is_local_choice`: local iff the memory node was a minimal-distance choice from the executing node — honest on any topology; unknown nodes never counted) plus `numa_nodes` (the map's node count; 1 = the whole machinery is structurally a no-op). The N-topology-general nearest-resource map lives in `crates/squeezefs-ipc/src/numa_core.rs` (`#[path]`-shared into the root crate and fuse3 per the `thp.rs` precedent, runtime-built from sysfs node distances/cpulists, injectable in tests — contracts in `tests/numa_affinity_tests.rs` cover single-node/2×1:1/NPS-4-node/CXL shapes); placement actions (session-arena `mbind`+populate composed BEFORE the THP collapse, CPU-weighted owner→node partition + locality-first session admission, service-thread + fuse3 queue-worker node pins ∩ process mask, node-targeted handler-lane handoffs `tpc_spawn_on_node`, per-queue payload-arena node binding) are all gated by **`SQUEEZEFS_NUMA=0`** (the A/B lever — the instrument stays alive on both sides) and are structurally inert on single-node machines; the ipc op-economy campaign (2026-07-28, `.benchmarks/2026-07-28-ipc-op-economy.md`) adds the completion-doorbell pair `ipc_cqe_wake_{writes,elided}` (the `transport_wake_*` discipline for the completion direction: wakes are paid only toward parked reapers — `writes/(writes+elided) ≈ 1` under a saturated reaping client means the parked gate stopped eliding; the same campaign made the warm §5.5.1 serve prelude allocation-free, pinned by `tests/ipc_op_economy_tests.rs`); the **wake-economy campaign** (design `docs/design-il-wake-economy.md`; PR 1 landed instruments-first 2026-08-14) adds `ipc_cqe_wake_collapsed` (the PR 2 wake-collapse latch's elisions — mark-passed completions toward a parked reaper whose FUTEX_WAKE the per-era latch elided; when PR 2 lands the wake-economy gauge's denominator becomes `writes/(writes+elided+collapsed)`; **the latch is MARK-VALUED since 2026-09-06** — `wake_paid_mark` records the mark the era's pay satisfied and a completion collapses iff it reads that same mark, with NO parker-side clear: the v3 0/1 flag + clear lost a wake when an already-REAPED op's late doorbell completion (the daemon publishes DONE, then bumps) won the era's CAS toward a parker not yet asleep, and the parker's real completion collapsed — a strand to the reap park's age bound, a latency tail never a hang; loom `ipc_cqe_latched_reaped_prior_completion_never_strands` with futex-bucket fidelity is the pin, `.benchmarks/2026-09-06-cqe-doorbell-lost-wake.md` the record; IPC_ABI unchanged — no layout change, KD-7 guards the word's meaning) and `ipc_cqe_pass_wake_flushes` (the decision-gated PR 4 pass-scoped wake flush; `flushes ≤ sessions × passes` by construction) — both **structurally 0 until their mechanisms land**, and `.benchmarks/rigs/2026-08-14-wake-economy-rig.sh` is the campaign's per-row instrument (PSI + wakes/s + gauge + engagement columns, pre-fill rule built in); the L3 transport-economy program (2026-07-18) adds `transport_wake_{writes,elided}` — queue-eventfd wake coalescing (lever B): `writes/(writes+elided) ≈ 1` under saturated load means the coalescer stopped eliding (evidence `.benchmarks/2026-07-18-l3-transport-economy.md`); the killpriv-v2 campaign (2026-07-28, `.benchmarks/2026-07-28-fuse-killpriv-v2.md`) adds `fuse_killpriv_negotiated` (0/1 per mount — 1 ⇒ `FUSE_HANDLE_KILLPRIV_V2` accepted at INIT: the kernel's per-write(2) `GETXATTR("security.capability")` killpriv probe is gone and the daemon owns the clearing law — suid always, sgid only if group-exec, caps xattr drop; 0 = pre-5.11 kernel or the testing-only `SQUEEZEFS_FUSE_NO_KILLPRIV=1` escape) and `fuse_killpriv_clears` (clears performed across flagged WRITE / O_TRUNC OPEN / SETATTR and the il-parity ring writes; steady growth on workloads that never touch priv'd files = known-clean-latch regression); the random-small-write program's families (`patch_*` incl. the `patch_ineligible_*` decision ledger and the `patch_edge_rmw_reads`/`write_path_seed_read_bytes` must-stay-0 tripwires, `active_block_ooo_runs`, `write_through_blocks`, `overwrite_seed_*`, `parked_extent_bytes`/`parked_full_buffer_bytes`, `extent_*`, `fold_*`, `staged_rider_*`, `extent_records_*`, `compress_stored_raw`) are listed under **Random-small-write program (Implemented 2026-07)**; the volume-lifecycle program's families (`job_*` incl. `job_remote_*`, `evacuate_*`, `volume_preflight_refusals`, `placement_table_refreshes` / `backend_placement_picks` / `backend_fill_{ratio,spread}`, `fsck_*` incl. the must-stay-0 `fsck_findings` and the repair/quarantine counters, `scrub_*`, `defrag_*` / `frag_d1..d4`, `fuse_reserved_xattr_refusals`) are listed under **Volume lifecycle & online maintenance program (Implemented 2026-07)**; the write-pipeline-depth campaign (2026-07-27, `src/write_pipeline.rs`, `.benchmarks/2026-07-27-write-pipeline-depth.md`) adds `write_pipeline_{inflight_blocks,inflight_bytes,depth_target,admission_waits,fence_drops}` — the internal depth instrument (the rig's iostat `aqu-sz` is the external one; the 2026-07-31 write-wall campaign adds the ALWAYS-ON `write_pipeline_phase_ns` ten-phase residence histograms — admit_wait / detach_lag / lock_wait / crypto / allocate / dma / publish / displaced_free / inval_tail / total, ungated on the stats inode so field mounts can name residence terms without a remount: the fresh-wall kill instrument, `.benchmarks/2026-07-31-write-wall.md`): `depth_target` is the runtime-derived BDP target (NO fixed depth anywhere — user directive; A/B lever `SQUEEZEFS_WRITE_PIPELINE_DEPTH_BLOCKS`, measurement only), `admission_waits` is the honest-writer-backpressure gauge, `fence_drops` is a must-stay-0 tripwire (investigate alongside `writer_guard_fenced`), and in-flight bytes ride the R5 budget as the non-sheddable `write_pipeline_inflight` component (Red clamps the admission target to its floor — converge by completion, never OOM); the write-commit-economy campaign (2026-07-30, `.benchmarks/2026-07-30-write-commit-economy.md` — per-ino publish coalescing + layout delta records: the O(file-size)-per-publish journal term collapsed to O(batch)) adds `layout_publish_{batches,batched_blocks}` (the lever-1 engagement instrument — blocks/batch is the live coalesce factor; ≈ 1 on a streaming shape means the publish conveyor regressed to the serialized per-block posture) and `layout_{delta,full}_commits` / `layout_delta_{bytes,folds}` (lever-2 engagement: streaming publishes onto a persisted inline base must grow `layout_delta_commits`, and `layout_delta_bytes` vs `meta_kv_journal_bytes` is the collapsed-byte instrument; knobs `SQUEEZEFS_PUBLISH_COALESCE_MAX` default 64 / `SQUEEZEFS_LAYOUT_DELTA_MAX_CHAIN` default 64 — `1`/`0` are the A/B levers, never operational escapes; on-disk face: `KV_LAYOUT_DELTAS` = superblock incompat bit 5, stamped+barriered before a volume's first delta record, never on untouched volumes); the rewrite-publish-drain campaign (2026-08-01, `.benchmarks/2026-08-01-rewrite-publish-drain.md` — the 7.7× rewrite publish tax attributed to commit-rate-coupled queueing at a ~92 %-utilized M7 conveyor, NOT rewrite mechanics or the journal device) adds the ALWAYS-ON `publish_phase_ns` decomposition (queue_wait / lock_wait / base_fetch / apply / save_encode / blob_write / meta_commit + the commit interior `commit_{guard,inode_read,slot_probe,tx_wait}` / total) and `meta_txpass_phase_ns` (the M7 pass interior: tx_queue_wait + pass_{admission,leaf_locks,journal_write,total}), the **base-provenance ledger** `publish_base_{dirty_serves,ram_serves,fetches}` (closes EXACTLY against the pass count; steady rewrite must run ram_serves ≈ passes, fetches ≈ first-touch — Lever A: a clean cached entry whose `layout_base_token` matches the ino's CURRENT fencing era serves as the RMW base, foreign eras refetch), the full-save decision ledger `publish_full_save_{indirect,chain_cap,other}` (chain_cap ≈ delta_commits/`SQUEEZEFS_LAYOUT_DELTA_MAX_CHAIN` is the restored re-base cadence), `layout_indirect_map_read{s,_bytes}` + `publish_indirect_blob_bytes` (the big-file publish faces, structurally 0 on inline-map shapes), and the Lever B aggregation pair `publish_commit_group{s,_saves}` (delta-class layout saves drain into ONE multi-ino KvTx per per-volume conveyor window — one journal entry/ring write/fan-out per window; saves/groups is the live aggregation factor; knob `SQUEEZEFS_PUBLISH_COMMIT_GROUP_MAX` — default derives from `SQUEEZEFS_META_COMMIT_BATCH_TXS`, `1` = the pre-campaign per-save commit path, the A/B lever); the probe-up governor campaign (2026-07-29, `.benchmarks/2026-07-29-probe-up-governor.md` — the pure-BDP target is a self-fulfilling equilibrium: the field left +18 % behind at its own converged depth) adds `write_pipeline_depth_target_base` (the un-probed BDP target — current-vs-base is the live probe contribution) and `write_pipeline_depth_probe_{ups,backoffs}` (BBR-flavored probe engagement vs dead-gain retreats: probes launch only on saturated epochs with R5 headroom, adopt only when delivery responds, retreat + duty-cycle cool down when marginal gain dies, and the multiplier bleeds to 1.0 on unsaturated epochs — `ups` stays 0 on latency-sensitive/low-offered-load mounts by design).
Mounted volumes expose process metrics under the virtual **stats** inode (JSON), including layout mix, bg admission, uring queue-full, and lease acquire outcomes. The 2026-08 frontier/rewrite wave adds: the **zcrx lane** family (`zcrx_fill_bytes`, `zcrx_gather_bytes`, `zcrx_lane_poisoned` must-stay-0, area/violation gauges — engagement law `gather ≡ fill`, closure byte-exact; `docs/design-zcrx-read-lane.md`), the **fuse3 transport-geometry/zc** gauges (`transport_max_write`/`transport_max_pages` — the negotiated-geometry law that replaced the hardcoded 256-page plan; `transport_max_readahead` — the kernel's readahead limit echoed VERBATIM at INIT and DELIBERATELY independent of the R2 prefetch window, FUSE-4d; `fuse3_kmbuf_negotiated` 0/1, `fuse3_zc_replies`, and the `commit_flush` fifth phase of `read/write_transport_phase_ns`), the **pre-RC transport/safety tripwires** (all must-stay-0: `transport_cq_overflows` with the `transport_cq_nodrop` capability probe — dropped ring completions, FUSE-3f; `read_dest_overruns` — zero-copy read destinations refused because the serve exceeded the transport's registered window, FUSE-4e; `open_count_stranded` — an inode reaching its FINAL forget with open handles still counted, i.e. a lost RELEASE, POSIX-14 ⊕ FUSE-3k's lookup refcount; `ipc_direct_reap_stalls` — direct-drive reaper enter failures observed with DMA in flight, MEM-7c), and the **rewrite program P0** families (`rewrite_{blocks,user_bytes,device_write_bytes}` — the vehicle-blind `rewrite_amp` SLO attribution, gates ≤1.05 + zero mid-row discards; `rewrite_shadow_{swaps,bytes,fallbacks,fence_drops,open_epochs}` with `fence_drops` must-stay-0 and `open_epochs`→0 at quiesce; the discard-elision debt ledger whose law is `queued + elided ≡ terminal frees`; supersession engagement counters — `docs/design-rewrite-program.md`).
