the volume-lifecycle program's families (`job_*` incl. `job_remote_*`, `evacuate_*`, `volume_preflight_refusals`, `placement_table_refreshes` / `backend_placement_picks` / `backend_fill_{ratio,spread}`, `fsck_*` incl. the must-stay-0 `fsck_findings` and the repair/quarantine counters, `scrub_*`, `defrag_*` / `frag_d1..d4`, `fuse_reserved_xattr_refusals`) are listed under **Volume lifecycle & online maintenance program (Implemented 2026-07)**; the write-pipeline-depth campaign (2026-07-27, `src/write_pipeline.rs`, `.benchmarks/2026-07-27-write-pipeline-depth.md`) adds `write_pipeline_{inflight_blocks,inflight_bytes,depth_target,admission_waits,fence_drops}` — the internal depth instrument (the rig's iostat `aqu-sz` is the external one; the 2026-07-31 write-wall campaign adds the ALWAYS-ON `write_pipeline_phase_ns` ten-phase residence histograms — admit_wait / detach_lag / lock_wait / crypto / allocate / dma / publish / displaced_free / inval_tail / total, ungated on the stats inode so field mounts can name residence terms without a remount: the fresh-wall kill instrument, `.benchmarks/2026-07-31-write-wall.md`): `depth_target` is the runtime-derived BDP target (NO fixed depth anywhere — user directive; A/B lever `SQUEEZEFS_WRITE_PIPELINE_DEPTH_BLOCKS`, measurement only), `admission_waits` is the honest-writer-backpressure gauge, `fence_drops` is a must-stay-0 tripwire (investigate alongside `writer_guard_fenced`), and in-flight bytes ride the R5 budget as the non-sheddable `write_pipeline_inflight` component (Red clamps the admission target to its floor — converge by completion, never OOM); the write-commit-economy campaign (2026-07-30, `.benchmarks/2026-07-30-write-commit-economy.md` — per-ino publish coalescing + layout delta records: the O(file-size)-per-publish journal term collapsed to O(batch)) adds `layout_publish_{batches,batched_blocks}` (the lever-1 engagement instrument — blocks/batch is the live coalesce factor; ≈ 1 on a streaming shape means the publish conveyor regressed to the serialized per-block posture) and `layout_{delta,full}_commits` / `layout_delta_{bytes,folds}` (lever-2 engagement: streaming publishes onto a persisted inline base must grow `layout_delta_commits`, and `layout_delta_bytes` vs `meta_kv_journal_bytes` is the collapsed-byte instrument; knobs `SQUEEZEFS_PUBLISH_COALESCE_MAX` default 64 / `SQUEEZEFS_LAYOUT_DELTA_MAX_CHAIN` default 64 — `1`/`0` are the A/B levers, never operational escapes; on-disk face: `KV_LAYOUT_DELTAS` = superblock incompat bit 5, stamped+barriered before a volume's first delta record, never on untouched volumes); the rewrite-publish-drain campaign (2026-08-01, `.benchmarks/2026-08-01-rewrite-publish-drain.md` — the 7.7× rewrite publish tax attributed to commit-rate-coupled queueing at a ~92 %-utilized M7 conveyor, NOT rewrite mechanics or the journal device) adds the ALWAYS-ON `publish_phase_ns` decomposition (queue_wait / lock_wait / base_fetch / apply / save_encode / blob_write / meta_commit + the commit interior `commit_{guard,inode_read,slot_probe,tx_wait}` / total) and `meta_txpass_phase_ns` (the M7 pass interior: tx_queue_wait + pass_{admission,leaf_locks,journal_write,total}), the **base-provenance ledger** `publish_base_{dirty_serves,ram_serves,fetches}` (closes EXACTLY against the pass count; steady rewrite must run ram_serves ≈ passes, fetches ≈ first-touch — Lever A: a clean cached entry whose `layout_base_token` matches the ino's CURRENT fencing era serves as the RMW base, foreign eras refetch), the full-save decision ledger `publish_full_save_{indirect,chain_cap,other}` (chain_cap ≈ delta_commits/`SQUEEZEFS_LAYOUT_DELTA_MAX_CHAIN` is the restored re-base cadence), `layout_indirect_map_read{s,_bytes}` + `publish_indirect_blob_bytes` (the big-file publish faces, structurally 0 on inline-map shapes), and the Lever B aggregation pair `publish_commit_group{s,_saves}` (delta-class layout saves drain into ONE multi-ino KvTx per per-volume conveyor window — one journal entry/ring write/fan-out per window; saves/groups is the live aggregation factor; knob `SQUEEZEFS_PUBLISH_COMMIT_GROUP_MAX` — default derives from `SQUEEZEFS_META_COMMIT_BATCH_TXS`, `1` = the pre-campaign per-save commit path, the A/B lever); the probe-up governor campaign (2026-07-29, `.benchmarks/2026-07-29-probe-up-governor.md` — the pure-BDP target is a self-fulfilling equilibrium: the field left +18 % behind at its own converged depth) adds `write_pipeline_depth_target_base` (the un-probed BDP target — current-vs-base is the live probe contribution) and `write_pipeline_depth_probe_{ups,backoffs}` (BBR-flavored probe engagement vs dead-gain retreats: probes launch only on saturated epochs with R5 headroom, adopt only when delivery responds, retreat + duty-cycle cool down when marginal gain dies, and the multiplier bleeds to 1.0 on unsaturated epochs — `ups` stays 0 on latency-sensitive/low-offered-load mounts by design).

**Pre-RC hardening families** (the 2026-08 spec §7/§11/§12 wave — every one verified against `src/fuse_client.rs`'s stats JSON): the **must-stay-0 tripwire set** `data_dma_fence_refusals` (RES-6 — data-plane DMA submissions refused because this mount's D0 writer guard is fenced/fail-stopped; the write-path twin of `block_free_reclaim_fence_halts`, investigate alongside `writer_guard_fenced`), `job_worker_panics` (RES-7 — a job execution UNWOUND; the worker survives and the record flips `Failed` loudly, but the panic is a bug), `detached_task_panics` (RES-8 — a detached data-path task unwound; nothing joins one, so this counter is the only record its work was lost), `invariant_tripwires` (RES-22 — runtime concurrency-outcome violations reported through `crate::note_invariant_tripwire` instead of a `debug_assert!` panic inside a handler task, the class that produced a lost-reply stall — the `transport_lease_overlong` precedent), and `writeback_errors_latched` (POSIX-16 close-time error reporting — the errseq equivalent: growth means close-time data loss IS being reported to applications, which is the point, and something upstream is failing). Plus: `data_volume_write_cache` (DUR-2 — the DATA plane's hardware durability face beside the metadata one, `<backing_dev>=<class>` per distinct data device; `write-back` means acknowledged writes stay volatile until `NvmeBlockDev::flush` completes, the barrier the fsync path issues), `transport_unparked_commits` (the §5.4 park ledger's closure half — `parked − unparked` at quiesce is the WEDGE count; parking itself is the re-arm gate WORKING, so closure is the invariant, not absence — TEST-7 #1's fix; since 2026-09-08 — the generic/795 wedge, `.benchmarks/2026-09-08-generic-795-lookup-wedge.md`, two delivered LOOKUPs whose handlers had REPLIED sat unpumped for 23 min while every worker parked unbounded — the queue worker's park is BOUNDED (100 ms, the zc bridge law generalized) while its drain group owes ANY reply, and `transport_park_tick_{commit,cqe}_rescues` are the lost-wake tripwires — what the pass after a tick found that a wake should have delivered, ≈ 0 on a healthy mount; `SQUEEZEFS_TEST_DROP_COMMIT_WAKES` is the seam, `tests/commit_wake_loss_tests.rs` the contract), `lease_retry_waits` / `lease_retry_exhaustions` (the POSIX-5 retry ladder: waits lost then won on a later attempt vs. the ladder giving up — growth on the first with 0 on the second is the ladder working), `lseek_holes_reported` (POSIX-2 sparse-export engagement — 0 across a `cp --sparse`/`tar -S` run means no tool ever learned about a hole), and the POSIX-4 pair `meta_parent_scans` / `readdir_parent_memo_hits` (reverse-dentry SCANS — the unindexed O(total dentries) walk, legitimate only on the `open_by_handle_at` reconnect path — vs `..` syntheses served from the parent memo; scans growing per `readdir` means every `ls`/`find`/`du` is paying the scan per directory again). R5 gained the eviction-channel components `read_lru_evict_channel` / `hot_block_evict_channel` (floor 0 — warmth is the cheapest sacrifice — weight 1, and a real shed that clamps SEND-side admission: parked victims belong to the draining worker and are never torn; only caches that ARMED a dehydration receiver park anything, which is why `write_lru` has no channel). **DLM stage S4** (spec §6.9; `src/dlm_slot.rs`, design note `docs/design-dynamic-meta-routing.md` §9) adds the lock-authority trio `dlm_mode` (`solo` = this node is the lock master for every metadata slot — the only shipped mode; lock objects home on the durable slot map via `route_ino_width`, so the lock master and the metadata authority are the same process by construction), `dlm_rpcs` (**must stay 0** in solo mode — by construction, since a foreign home is refused loud at the site the remote acquire will occupy, never granted locally) and `dlm_term` (the process-wide durable writer era every fencing token this mount mints carries; the per-volume face is `writer_guard_term`). **DLM S7** (`src/data_custody.rs`; contracts `tests/dlm_data_fence_tests.rs`, guarantee rows in `docs/operations.md` §The DATA plane) adds the data plane's custody-epoch face: `data_dma_epoch_refusals` (the CLASS SPLIT of the `data_dma_fence_refusals` tripwire — submissions refused because the custody epoch they were authorized under is no longer current, i.e. custody MOVED under an in-flight authorization, as opposed to this mount's own D0 latch firing; ⊆ its parent, must stay 0), `dlm_quarantined_offsets` / `dlm_quarantine_releases` (the dead-epoch **do-not-reallocate** quarantine — admission takes the offset out of the free list, a quarantined offset's `finish_free` DEFERS its publish, and only a drain proof releases it: the landed WERO preempt of the dead epoch's host on a PR substrate, recovery's proof of death otherwise; a gauge that never falls is honestly-unavailable space, never a leak — allocation refuses `StorageFull` rather than hand a possibly-live zombie's offset to a new owner), and `data_plane_fence_mode` (1 = a WERO rtype-3 reservation is held on every data namespace, so a fenced host's DMA is rejected by the DEVICE — the data-plane twin of `writer_guard_mode`; the opt-in is `SQUEEZEFS_MULTI_WRITER=1`, which REFUSES the mount loud on a non-PR substrate or an unstamped format — incompat bit 11, stamped by the default format since the rung-10b flip; `--single-writer`/pre-flip volumes upgrade offline). The authorization point is ONE function (`data_custody::authorize_dma`): every DMA submission passes it, `write_block_authorized` is the form that carries an epoch captured when custody was established (the write-pipeline permit is the first carrier), and the device-side rejection half is the DEFERRED leg specified in `docs/design-nvmeof-target-management.md` §6.8.1. **DLM S5 — the reader families** (`docs/operations.md` §Read-only coherent mounts): the posture `read_only_mount` plus the two DERIVED numbers `reader_revalidate_interval_ms` / `reader_staleness_bound_ms` (= interval + the writer's ≤1 s checkpoint ceiling — published so the guarantee in the docs cannot drift from the one in force; 0 on write mounts, and **0 on a reader under READ TOKENS** — PR 5, where the poll is the control plane only), and the activity family `meta_kv_revalidate_{polls,epochs,nodes_dropped,stale_serves,dirty_skips,keys_purged}` + `meta_kv_reader_load_retries` / `meta_kv_node_partition_refusals` — ALL 0 for the life of a write mount, so nonzero values ARE the statement "this mount is a coherent reader". Two are must-stay-0 on every posture (`dirty_skips` = revalidation armed on a mount that writes; `partition_refusals` = an appender reached for a node population it does not own), and two are INVERTED tripwires now that the mount installs the census purge sink: `epochs` advancing while `nodes_dropped` stays flat means the drop pass finds nothing to drop, and `epochs` advancing on a mount with cached blocks while `keys_purged` stays flat means the R-6 purge is not reaching the tiers — each a coherence promise silently not kept, never an unwired one. **DLM stage S8** (spec §6.7 decision 1 + §6.9 S8; `src/meta_ship/`, operator surface `docs/operations.md` §Metadata function shipping) adds the `meta_ship` object — metadata authority is OWNABLE, not lockable, so a verb on a volume another node owns is function-shipped to that owner: `armed` (false on every shipped mount — nothing arms the plane until S9's multi-writer mount), the routing ledger `local_verbs`/`shipped_verbs`/`served_verbs` (a client's shipped count must equal the owner's served count — the engagement law), the pipelining pair `batches`/`batched_verbs` (verbs÷batches is the live coalesce factor; ≈ 1 on a serial stream, which is §6.10 R1's accepted cost made visible rather than hidden), `dedup_hits` (replays answered from the owner's `(client_epoch, request_id)` window instead of double-applying), `retries`, `stale_term_refusals` (issued AS AN OWNER) vs `era_relearns` (observed AS A CLIENT — split so a node that is both cannot double-count one failover), `cross_owner_refusals` (a `rename`/`link` spanning two owners, refused loud with EXDEV naming S3.5 — the per-volume-owner recipe's arm; **0 by construction on a symmetric mount since PR 6**, where a cross-holder op is one intent with shipped steps), `not_owner_refusals`, `mint_redirects` (a create redirected to an owned volume — §6.10 R4's one-appender-per-volume constraint engaging), `dlm_rpcs_meta` (the metadata face of the round-trip ledger; `dlm_rpcs` keeps its S4 meaning — LOCK round trips — untouched), the failover pair `dlm_grace_reclaims`/`dlm_grace_conflicts` (a successor's grace window admits reclaim and refuses fresh mutations; conflicts stay 0 on a healthy failover), and the `dlm_token_cache_*` family — S8's discharge of the S4 fencing-read contract, where `dlm_token_cache_misses` is a **must-stay-0 tripwire** (a miss means a fencing read reached a foreign-home object without the metadata RPC that carries its grant) alongside `owner_panics`. `meta_ship_phase_ns` (route/queue_wait/encode/rtt/decode) and `meta_ship_owner_phase_ns` (admit/dispatch/execute/reply_encode/total) are the always-on attribution the deferred `tar -x` A/B needs (rulings D10/D11). **The owner's dispatch executes on the accepting connection's thread since D-5 (2026-09-08, `.benchmarks/2026-09-08-d5-owner-hop-and-depth.md`; e2e perf audit DLM #7/#8):** every owner-side dispatch on both planes goes through ONE door (`meta_ship::owner_dispatch`) that records `meta_ship_owner_dispatch_ns` {`queue_hop`, `run`, `wake_hop`, `total`} exact-sum and polls the work inline (`SQUEEZEFS_META_SHIP_INLINE_SERVE` — both hops 0 by construction under `1`; **ships OFF**: the 2026-09-08 squeeze-test fleet row (`.benchmarks/2026-09-08-d5-fleet-squeeze-test.md`, two same-binary A-B-B-A brackets, both orders) found the served work 0.6–0.9 ms SLOWER on the connection thread than the two hops it deletes (co-writer publish latency +15–49 %, ingest −1.5…−7 %) — the in-process win needed an artificial lane hog, the field's lanes run at ρ ≈ 0.2; `0` = the shipped hop; engagement `meta_ship.owner_dispatch_{inline,hops}`), the owner's session is a lane parked on its socket (read-ahead — a pipelining peer's calls are served concurrently on one connection), the publish depth CAN ride one pipelined session per authority (`SQUEEZEFS_PUBLISH_SHIP_MULTIPLEX=1`; `MuxSession`, `meta_ship_publish.ship_mux_frames` — ships OFF on measurement: the connection thread is the execution venue, so one session serves K frames on one owner thread where the pool serves them on K, and the pool ran ≈ 1.5× at equal depth in-process; the capability arm for a fleet whose connection cap binds before its CPU), the accept thread waits on the listening socket instead of sleeping its tick (dial 100 ms → 0.2 ms), and both wire sockets run `TCP_NODELAY`. **DLM S6 — membership off the journal** (`src/membership.rs` + `src/membership_wire.rs` + `src/membership_sim.rs`; contracts `tests/dlm_membership_tests.rs`; operator surface `docs/operations.md` §Membership plane) adds the `membership_*` family: liveness is a RAM lease renewed over `cluster_wire`, so `membership_renewals` (the heartbeat) grows while `meta_kv_journal_entries` does **not** — that non-growth IS the S6 gate (§6.5 item 3 measured the `client:{uuid}` beat plane serializing 455 journal beats/s against the 1,500/s 15 k clients need) — while `membership_registration_commits` is bounded by mounts and membership CHANGES, so growth proportional to renewals is the regression; `membership_self_fences` is the §6.7 stricter-client-clock instrument (`T_self = T_owner − 2·skew_max − D_purge`, anchored on the member's own send instant: a member fail-stops its own objects BEFORE the owner can re-grant — must stay 0, and an unsafe clock configuration REFUSES the plane rather than clamping); `membership_evictions` mints the S7 dead epoch its offsets are quarantined under; `membership_grace_{refusals,reclaims}` gauge the owner-failover re-assertion window (reclaim admitted, conflicting fresh acquires refused); `membership_census_serves` is the read side that replaced `listxattr(1)` + one `getxattr` per client under `I{1}`; plus the gauges `membership_mode` (`off` — the shipped default until `SQUEEZEFS_MEMBERSHIP_BIND` is set — / `owner` / `member`), `membership_{members,readers,writers}`, `membership_{lease_ttl,self_deadline,grace_remaining}_ms` and `membership_min_acked_free_epoch` (**the exact API §6.8 item 3 calls**: `MemberSession::ack_free_epoch` → `MembershipOwner::{min_acked_free_epoch,members_behind_free_epoch,evict}` — item 3 itself is BUILT on it, `src/free_grace.rs`, described below). §6.2 **item 7**'s claim-set record rides incompat **bit 14** (`KV_CLAIM_SET`, stamped by the default format since the rung-10b flip): un-engaged volumes — and any volume without the bit — read the singleton PROJECTION of `writer_claim`, so single-writer records stay byte-identical, and the record's NVMe registrant keys join S7's shared `WeroHold` rather than forking a second reservation. Readers become visible in `squeezefs clients` for the first time (kinds `member-reader`/`member-writer`) at the cost of zero metadata writes — the S5 gap — and D0's write exclusion rows are untouched: the plane answers *who is here*, never *who may write*. **DLM stage S9 — the multi-writer data plane** (spec §6.9 S9; `src/data_grant.rs` + `src/multi_writer.rs` + `src/meta_ship/publish.rs`; contracts `tests/dlm_multi_writer_tests.rs`; operator surface `docs/operations.md` §Multi-writer data plane) adds the **remote write-custody** ledger as the `dlm_custody` object — `mode` (`off` on every shipped mount / `authority` / `co-writer` / `both`), `dlm_custody_held`, `dlm_custody_{grants,renewals,releases,conflicts}`, `dlm_custody_via_slot_holder` / `dlm_custody_token_carried` (symmetric PR 9 — under the ARMED plane the custody SERVER for a file is its slot HOLDER, not the set authority: grants served by a holder, and those whose reply carried the file's token; 0 unarmed and for every own file), `dlm_revokes_{issued,expired}`, `dlm_custody_unknown_leases` (the **pull-based** revocation channel firing: this plane has no push backchannel, so a revoked co-writer learns at its next renewal and the window is bounded by its own `T_self` self-fence and by the device, never by its belief), `dlm_custody_self_fences`, `dlm_custody_{quarantined_offsets,drain_proofs}`, `dlm_custody_grace_conflicts` (**must stay 0** on a healthy failover — the S6/S8 `dlm_grace_conflicts` law for this plane), and the opt-in `dlm_custody_grant_census` (VAL-7a's law: it names peer identities, inos and byte ranges, so it rides `SQUEEZEFS_STATS_KEY_CENSUS`; the count always exports) — plus the epoch pair `dlm_custody_generation` / `dlm_custody_epoch_advances` (S7's epoch gained its per-client low bits: an advance retires every DMA authorization minted under the previous generation and **never poisons**, which is why `data_dma_epoch_refusals` growth is read beside it), the always-on `dlm_custody_phase_ns` (rtt / arbitrate / adopt / renew — the deferred fan-out row's attribution), and the publish ledger `meta_ship_publish.{shipped,local,served,refusals,owner_panics}` where **`refusals` and `owner_panics` must stay 0** (a refusal means the ownership plane was armed without its publish half — a foreign-home publish refuses rather than appending to a peer's journal ring). **The publish plane FRAMES since D-1b (e2e perf audit DLM board #8, 2026-09-02, `.benchmarks/2026-09-02-d1b-publish-plane-batching.md`)**: `PublishRequestFrame` carries N calls (schema 13 — KD-7 same-commit fleets), the client keeps S8's lane shape per authority (a frame is whatever is queued when a frame slot frees — no timer, no added delay on a serial stream) with `SQUEEZEFS_PUBLISH_SHIP_DEPTH` frames in flight on that many sessions (derived `clamp(ceil(cpus/8), 2, 8)` — the owner RPC-lane slope; `1` = the stop-and-wait A/B control), and the owner partitions a frame into D-1's dependency chains so independent inos co-queue into ONE M7 pass while same-ino calls keep submission order; every gate (era, not-owner, witness, RETRIED/REFUSED class, `recomputed`) is PER CALL (`PublishCallOutcome`), and a frame is homogeneous in resend class so a transport resend re-sends the same request ids or nothing. Ledger: `ship_frames` / `ship_framed_calls` (calls ÷ frames = the live coalesce factor, ≈ 1 on a serial stream by design), `ship_depth_waits` (the K+1'th frame parking — saturation at the current depth), `ship_session_dials` (≤ depth per endpoint at steady state; growth = sessions dying between frames), `served_frames` / `served_frame_calls` / `served_chains` (chains ÷ frames ≈ frame width = independent, ≈ 1 = one hot object). In-process (release): 24 concurrent publishes went 24 frames / 24 passes / 122 µs each → 1–5 frames / 3–5 passes / 26 µs each; local fleet A-B-B-A (8 co-writers × 24 streams, tcp devsub): frames/publish 1.00 → 0.61–0.63, owner passes −39 %, journal entries −24 %, per-block publish latency −74 %, aggregate ingest PAR — the row is CPU-bound and the authority's conveyor reaches ρ ≈ 0.97 on the fix (DLM #2's two-stage conveyor is the next wall). **The owner commits a frame as ONE conveyor group since D-1c (§5.3 row 1's residual rung, 2026-09-04, `.benchmarks/2026-09-04-d1c-conveyor-group-per-frame.md` — field A-B-B-A owed)**: since C-2's instant-drain pass a frame's calls reached `commit_tx` at different instants and landed in 3–14 passes per 24 publishes; now the multi-chain dispatch proceeds in ROUNDS — every chain's next call, the round's `SetLayoutAndSize` calls PREPARED concurrently (kvmap probe, custody-scoped compose, over-cap ref chunks) under the round's serve stripes (deduped, ascending) and committed as one `RoutedMetaBackend::set_layout_and_size_group` (one `lock_many` per volume, one `commit_tx_group`), the round's other verbs served beside them, a chain's next call only after its previous finished — so a 24-distinct-ino frame is one round, one group, one pass BY CONSTRUCTION (`a_framed_burst_is_one_owner_conveyor_pass_by_construction` pins it without a held pass). Each grouped call CLAIMS its `(lease_epoch, request_id)` witness synchronously (`sqz_once::OnceCell::{claim_init,complete_init,abandon_init}`); a failed claim takes the serial path (parks on the winner, counts the replay). Lever `SQUEEZEFS_PUBLISH_CONVEYOR_GROUP` (default on; `0` = D-1b's per-chain dispatch, read per served frame); engagement `meta_ship_publish.frame_groups` beside `meta_conveyor_group_{commits,txs}`. Not yet grouped (the next rung): the S8 verb plane's `run_batch`, `MergeLayoutAndSize`/Lever-B, `FreeBlocks`, `RaiseAllocLane`, kvmap puts. Every field is 0/`off` on a single-writer mount BY CONSTRUCTION, and the arm (`SQUEEZEFS_MULTI_WRITER=1`, `SQUEEZEFS_MW_BIND`) refuses on a non-PR substrate, on a format missing any of the six capability bits 7/9/10/11/13/14 (stamped by the default format since the rung-10b flip; `--single-writer`/pre-flip volumes refuse until `volume enable-multi-writer` upgrades them offline), and on a mount whose membership plane is off; S9 takes **no new incompat bit**. **DLM S6 — membership off the journal** (`src/membership.rs` + `src/membership_wire.rs` + `src/membership_sim.rs`; contracts `tests/dlm_membership_tests.rs`; operator surface `docs/operations.md` §Membership plane) adds the `membership_*` family: liveness is a RAM lease renewed over `cluster_wire`, so `membership_renewals` (the heartbeat) grows while `meta_kv_journal_entries` does **not** — that non-growth IS the S6 gate (§6.5 item 3 measured the `client:{uuid}` beat plane serializing 455 journal beats/s against the 1,500/s 15 k clients need) — while `membership_registration_commits` is bounded by mounts and membership CHANGES, so growth proportional to renewals is the regression; `membership_self_fences` is the §6.7 stricter-client-clock instrument (`T_self = T_owner − 2·skew_max − D_purge`, anchored on the member's own send instant: a member fail-stops its own objects BEFORE the owner can re-grant — must stay 0, and an unsafe clock configuration REFUSES the plane rather than clamping); `membership_evictions` mints the S7 dead epoch its offsets are quarantined under; `membership_grace_{refusals,reclaims}` gauge the owner-failover re-assertion window (reclaim admitted, conflicting fresh acquires refused); `membership_census_serves` is the read side that replaced `listxattr(1)` + one `getxattr` per client under `I{1}`; plus the gauges `membership_mode` (`off` — the shipped default until `SQUEEZEFS_MEMBERSHIP_BIND` is set — / `owner` / `member`), `membership_{members,readers,writers}`, `membership_{lease_ttl,self_deadline,grace_remaining}_ms` and `membership_min_acked_free_epoch` (**the exact API §6.8 item 3 calls**: `MemberSession::ack_free_epoch` → `MembershipOwner::{min_acked_free_epoch,members_behind_free_epoch,evict}`). **§6.8 item 3 — the freed-offset grace period** (`src/free_grace.rs`, contracts `tests/reader_free_grace_tests.rs`, operator surface `docs/operations.md` §Freed-offset grace period) is BUILT on that channel and adds the `free_grace_*` family: the posture word `free_grace_mode` (`off` = no plane, the shipped default / `idle` = armed with no members / `armed`), the ledger `free_grace_{deferrals,releases,offsets,bytes}` whose closure law is `deferrals ≡ releases + offsets`, the reallocation bound `free_grace_bound` beside its derived numbers `free_grace_{fence_bound_ms,pressure_bound_ms,ring_cap}` (published so the operator page cannot drift from the machinery — since the pressure valve `fence_bound_ms` is the deadline **in force** and `free_grace_fence_bound_base_ms` the un-tightened derivation, the `write_pipeline_depth_target`/`_base` precedent), the reader-side `free_grace_reader_acks` (**flat while the writer churns IS the failure** — that reader is holding the writer's free list), and the two tripwires `free_grace_forced_releases` / `free_grace_laggard_fences` (**0 on a healthy fleet**: a forced release is the ONE arm that trades a reader's coherence for the writer's progress, and it only ever happens together with that reader's eviction — "fenced, not waited on") plus `free_grace_alloc_stalls` (the ENOSPC-not-corruption ruling's instrument: allocation refused rather than hand out an offset a reader may still resolve — silent on a passthrough volume). **The hold-time campaign (2026-09-06, `.benchmarks/2026-09-06-free-grace-hold-time.md` — finding 15's remaining half: with the supply leak closed the s11 row HOLDS the supply, `bound_age` 8,994 ms at every 1 Hz cadence) adds the decomposition `free_grace_hold_phase_ns` (`defer_checkpointed` / `checkpointed_min_acked` / `min_acked_released` / `total`, exact-sum per placed sample, read off the KV checkpoint mark and `publish_bound`'s own advance — `free_grace_hold_unplaced` counts the rest), `free_grace_hold_ms` (the live residence EWMA — the measured hold the capacity law `churn × hold + live ≤ cap/W` multiplies by; the co-writer publishes the law as `alloc_lane_share_needed_blocks` / `alloc_lane_headroom_pct`), `free_grace_checkpoint_{marks,cycle_ms}`, `free_grace_member_ack_lag_ms` (max/mean/min over members — the min-composition's culprit finder; the per-member census rides `SQUEEZEFS_STATS_KEY_CENSUS`), and two levers landed on in-process evidence (−922 ms of an 8,646 ms coupled hold, closure exact, zero fences): `SQUEEZEFS_FREE_GRACE_ACK_RENEWAL` (a promotion wakes the renewal loop and renews as CARRIAGE — the ack travels now, the beat and the label stay the routine renewal's; `free_grace_ack_renewals`) and `SQUEEZEFS_FREE_GRACE_REFRESH_ON_ACK` (a binding member's advancing ack marks the bound dirty, the next harvest recomputes it rate-limited to `floor ÷ members` / 2× the measured scan; `free_grace_bound_refreshes_on_ack`, `free_grace_bound_scan_ms`). The remaining 6.0 s of the hold is the two DERIVED reader windows (`S + skew`, `S + D_purge` — each term a `P` or the checkpoint ceiling), named as adjudication items in the note's §7; a faster writer checkpoint cadence alone is measured INERT (the reader qualifies on the time bound, not the event). **The ladder re-derivation (user decision 2026-09-06, `.benchmarks/2026-09-06-free-grace-ladder-rederivation.md`, KD-FG-11 amended) landed items 1–3 of that §7:** qualify = the WRITER's checkpoint landing ceiling + skew (`checkpoint_landing_ceiling_ms` = trigger + 2 ticks, 1,100 ms shipped, through the one accessor `MemberSession::checkpoint_ceiling_ms` — the poll interval bounded nothing, the pass IS the poll), the drain's `S` = an epoch-step invalidation of the reader's layout cache (`CachedMetadata::reader_step_gen` vs `ro_coherence::layout_entry_pre_step`, stamped BEFORE the backend read, one relaxed load per serve, refused at the handler resolve / binding recheck / il sync probe / dd prelude / fill tasks), its `D_purge` = an OBSERVED in-flight drain (`ro_coherence::ServeStamp` on every read serve — one `SeqCst` increment + one same-word decrement, per-thread shards, the Dekker pair with the step's bump — `serve_drained_below` at the qualifying pass or on the last straggler's completion wake; `D_purge` survives as the must-stay-0 `free_grace_drain_overdue`); levers `SQUEEZEFS_FREE_GRACE_{QUALIFY_CEILING,DRAIN_EPOCH_STAMP,DRAIN_OBSERVED}` (default on, each `0` = its retired term verbatim), gauges `free_grace_{qualify,drain}_lag_ms`, `free_grace_drain_{observed,overdue,wakes}`, `reader_layout_step_{gen,misses}`, `reader_serves_inflight`, `reader_serve_{step_races,slot_overruns}` (the last must stay 0); fleet-cadence model `bound_age` 7,724 → 2,724 ms, the fleet row owed. **The writer→member checkpoint composite (adjudication item 4, user decision 2026-09-06, `.benchmarks/2026-09-06-free-grace-checkpoint-composite.md`)** lands the cadence lever as the composite it pays as: while the valve is asking (a prod in force) the authority's KV checkpoint ceiling is `max(P/2, 2 × measured cycle)` against the reader's routine poll, the membership grant carries the ceiling in force (`Grant::checkpoint_ceiling_ms` — a promise honoured one routine ceiling past the grant; `CLUSTER_WIRE_SCHEMA` 1 → 2), and the member's prod floor, L2b pass floor and pipeline depth follow it — in-process `bound_age` 7,724 → 6,750 ms at exactly 2× checkpoints/s and 2× lease-lane renewals/s (the accepted cost); gauges `free_grace_checkpoint_ceiling_ms` / `free_grace_checkpoint_elastic_cycles`, lever `SQUEEZEFS_FREE_GRACE_CHECKPOINT_COMPOSITE` (`0` = the shipped cadences exactly), the published staleness bound unchanged. **Composed (the integration, same day): the grant advertises the LANDING ceiling of the decision in force (`checkpoint_landing_ceiling_for_elastic` = `c + 2 × min(tick, c)`; the routine `trigger + 2 × tick`), so `MemberSession::checkpoint_ceiling_ms()` — the qualify term’s and the pass floor’s one input — reads the advertised landing value with the member’s own landing derivation as the fallback; the member-side gauge is `free_grace_advertised_ceiling_ms`, distinct from the writer’s decision-ceiling gauge.** The **pressure-coupled release valve** (rung-20 residual 6, landed 2026-08-21) adds the graded signal `free_grace_pressure_pct` (0 quiet … 100 = the supply is gone at the ring's own measured deferral rate against the smaller of its headroom and the volume's free supply — a rate-derived forecast, never an occupancy) and the two rung gauges `free_grace_prods` (rung a — grants that carried a SHORTER renewal cadence to a member the writer is waiting on, the cadence itself published live as `free_grace_prod_renew_ms`; growth with `free_grace_bound` flat is the stop-and-read signal) and `free_grace_bound_tightenings` (rung b — harvests that evaluated a deadline below the routine bound; its ratio against `forced_releases` is the point of the valve, and both flat while `offsets` climbs means `SQUEEZEFS_FREE_GRACE_VALVE=0`). Rung (c) — the forced release with its eviction — is unchanged and still last, and the tightening's FLOOR is one honest acknowledgement cycle, so no rung can fence a reader that is answering as designed. The reader's ack ladder snapshots a CANDIDATE label (a renewal cadence below `staleness + skew_max` would otherwise re-learn the qualification target out from under every pass and the reader would acknowledge nothing for ever — the "climbs and never drains" signature, reachable without the valve at `SQUEEZEFS_META_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS=5000`). The gate is one relaxed load when unarmed, sits strictly DOWNSTREAM of the reclaim queue (`finish_free` is its only entry, so pop-ownership/fence-halt/manners are untouched), and composes with S7's dead-epoch quarantine custody-first: an offset released by a drain proof enters grace rather than the free list. Epoch identity is the owner's own monotonic instant echoed as a CAUSAL token — never the per-volume ledger sequence, whose min/max composition starves in an unbalanced set. Readers become visible in `squeezefs clients` for the first time (kinds `member-reader`/`member-writer`) at the cost of zero metadata writes — the S5 gap — and D0's write exclusion rows are untouched: the plane answers *who is here*, never *who may write*. **The renewal is also §6.8 item 3's acknowledgement CARRIER, and its liveness law is "never queue behind bulk work, on either side, and never wait a routine beat for a decision already taken"** (finding 15 phase B1, 2026-09-07, `.benchmarks/2026-09-07-membership-renewal-isolation.md`): the always-on `membership_renew_phase_ns` {`carry_wait`,`rtt`,`total`} (member) / `membership_renew_serve_ns` (authority) / `membership_renew_cadence_ms` (the beat in force) decide WHERE a slow renewal's time went instead of arguing it — on the day-2 fleet it was in none of them: the caught-up arm of `free_grace::take_prod_cadence` handed every member the routine 10 s beat at the instant the ring drained, and the beat is the member's only label source, so the refill two seconds later could be told to nobody for 10 s (`SQUEEZEFS_FREE_GRACE_CAUGHT_UP_RELAX`, default on: one doubling step instead, gauge `free_grace_prods_caught_up`); the renewal's socket round trip also left the shared `sqz-blk` FIFO for its own `sqz-lease-io` thread (`SQUEEZEFS_MEMBERSHIP_RENEW_LANE`, default on; `membership_renew_lane_calls`), completing finding 2's isolation at the wire. **DLM S9 blocker #3 — the data-plane allocation partition** (`src/data_alloc_lane.rs`; design `docs/design-mw-data-alloc-partition.md`; contracts `tests/mw_data_alloc_lane_tests.rs`; capacity planning `docs/operations.md` §Multi-writer capacity planning) adds the `alloc_lane_*` family: fresh block allocation is a per-writer **residue class** (`b % W`, `crate::lane_core`'s arithmetic reused verbatim from §6.2 item 5's ino lanes) while **frees stay lane-blind** — the owning lane is derivable from the OFFSET, so a writer frees a peer's block with no ownership lookup, no message and no record, and `begin_free`/`finish_free` are untouched. `alloc_lane_writers` is the partition width and **`0` = unpartitioned**, which is every mount today: a SOLO engagement installs nothing at all, so single-writer allocation is not merely equivalent to the shipped path but literally it (pinned, including zero durable commits and zero gauge movement). The rest: `alloc_lane_id`, `alloc_lanes_owned` (own + lanes ADOPTED after their holder was proven dead — the witness is S7's `DeadEpoch`, the same drain proof `release_quarantine` demands, and adoption needs no durable record because the reservation is keyed on the LANE, not the holder), `alloc_lane_reservations` (durable per-lane watermark commits — one per derived grain of **fresh** blocks, `SQUEEZEFS_ALLOC_LANE_RESERVE_BLOCKS`; **reuse pays none**, so the rewrite hot path adds zero), `alloc_lane_adoptions`, `alloc_lane_enospc_refusals` (**must stay 0** — a writer starving while the set has free space; the refusal names the unreachable free blocks and keeps the `StorageFull` class the ENOSPC valve keys on) and `alloc_lane_stranded_bytes` (the **published capacity bound** = `capacity − Σ owned lane shares ≤ cap × (W−1)/W + (W−1)`; the granularity cost when every writer stays inside its `cap/W ± 1` share is only `W − 1` blocks). Durability is one 21-byte versioned+checksummed `alloc_lane:{vol_tag}:{lane}` record on ino 1 (KD-2's plane, VAL-2-allowlist-invisible, `vol_tag` per KD-5) written AHEAD of use, because derived state cannot see a fenced-but-live predecessor's minted-and-unpublished tail; recovery is `max(reservation, derived floor, every foreign-WIDTH record)` rounded into the lane. Contiguity picks keep working WITHIN a lane (one stride coarser — the stated fragmentation cost), fsck C6 now declines to reconcile foreign lanes, and the partition takes **no new incompat bit**: it gates on bit 11, whose meaning already is "the format's recovery paths are expressed for more than one data-plane writer". **The ADMISSION landed with the co-writer lane grant** (`src/alloc_lane_grant.rs`, design §9a, contracts `tests/mw_cowriter_lane_tests.rs`), which is what makes a non-solo `alloc_lane_writers` reachable at all: the authority derives ONE lane per enrolled writer from the durable claim set (itself lane 0, width rounded up to a power of two because `AppendPartition` admits only those — so three writers run at `W = 4` and the unassigned lane joins `alloc_lane_stranded_bytes`), and carries `(writer_lane, writers)` to each co-writer on the S9 **custody lease** — never a knob, since two co-writers choosing lanes is the collision the partition exists to prevent, and never the co-writer's own derivation from the record, since a roster growth would give a later reader a wider width whose classes OVERLAP a live peer's. A member the arm-time snapshot does not name is refused at the custody JOIN (a width change is a new authority ERA), and a renewal that ever answered a different lane makes the mount self-fence. Because the reservation record is a metadata commit a co-writer cannot perform, its raise SHIPS (`PublishCall::RaiseAllocLane`, publish schema 2) and the AUTHORITY commits it — the client names a LANE and the owner derives the record name, checks the lane against the assignment it made under the lease epoch it minted, and holds the monotonicity (the frontier only ever rises) — with two new gauges: `alloc_lane_shipped_reservations` (the co-writer engagement instrument: all of a co-writer's raises travel, so 0 with a nonzero `alloc_lane_id` means a mount is committing its own frontier, which only an authority may do) and `alloc_lane_raise_refusals` (**must stay 0** — a lane/width/lease refusal, and any refusal means the offset was NOT handed out). A co-writer's lane is also OPENED by the authority before its first mint (a raise with `upto == 0` commits nothing and answers the recovery floor), because a co-writer runs no ownership-recovery walk and would otherwise mint from block 0 of a device whose low blocks are live. **A co-writer's PROACTIVE lane refills — the ahead-of-stall watermark tick (`SQUEEZEFS_ALLOC_LANE_HARVEST_AHEAD`) and the lane-push pushed refill (`SQUEEZEFS_FREE_GRACE_LANE_PUSH`, the renewal grant's `lane_supply_blocks` hint → `free_grace_lane_push_wakes` / `alloc_lane_pushed_harvests`) — gate on the authority's ADVERTISED lane supply (`free_grace_lane_supply_hint`, `free_grace::lane_supply_witnessed`), never on the owed ledger alone (the refill-hint gate, 2026-09-07, `.benchmarks/2026-09-07-lane-refill-hint-gate.md`: `alloc_lane_owed_blocks` is the EXPLICIT-ship arm's face and ≈ 90 % of a rewriting co-writer's displaced blocks return through the authority's publish recompute, which notes nothing owed — so the owed gate left both arms dark while the authority advertised hundreds of the lane's blocks and every refill ran from inside an ENOSPC park; `SQUEEZEFS_ALLOC_LANE_REFILL_HINT=0` is the owed-only A/B control, `alloc_lane_hint_refills` the engagement, and the ENOSPC-path harvest is unchanged). **Every harvest caller — the ENOSPC path, its parked retries, the placed allocation's failover, both proactive ticks — is SINGLE-FLIGHT per allocator since 2026-09-07 (finding 15 phase B1, `.benchmarks/2026-09-07-lane-harvest-single-flight.md`; `SQUEEZEFS_ALLOC_LANE_HARVEST_SINGLE_FLIGHT`, `0` = one RPC per caller): one in-flight RPC per (co-writer, volume) whose outcome every concurrent caller joins (`alloc_lane_harvest_coalesced`), a fresh empty reply declining re-issue until a grant / wake / owed `Freed` moves the advertisement (`alloc_lane_harvest_declined_stale` — the renewal cadence is the bound, never a timer), joiners bounded by the park wall — because the fleet's file-per-proc phase issued 124k harvests in 9.5 min, half empty — the storm the samples showed beside the renewal stall (whose own cause turned out to be the routine beat, next paragraph).** **Since the fpp-residue landing (2026-09-07, `.benchmarks/2026-09-07-cowriter-fpp-supply-residue.md`) the refill's decisions are PER VOLUME and the fpp refusals themselves are the capacity law (§8 of that note — the venue's `-k` keeps 640 of the lane's 1,024 blocks live through B1/B2/A2 against a ≈ 3.4 s recycle loop; A1, with 320 live, refuses nothing): the renewal grant carries the lane-supply hint per data volume (`Grant::lane_supply_volumes`, `CLUSTER_WIRE_SCHEMA` 3 — `SQUEEZEFS_ALLOC_LANE_VOLUME_HINT`, `alloc_lane_volume_hint_skips`), the single-flight decline's witness is the asking volume's advertisement, the supply-coupled epoch close stays MOUNT-wide (a per-volume plan was tried and retired the same day — under the lane-aware placement a co-writer's stock is fungible across its volumes, and the D row measured the per-volume plan stranding the covered sibling's parked keys to the iteration boundary), and `BackendRouter::lane_allocators` names the default slot's alias of the first volume once (one refill task per volume, not two).** **DLM S9 — the CO-WRITER mount posture** (§6.2 item 7's CONSUMER half; `src/cowriter.rs`; contracts `tests/dlm_cowriter_tests.rs`; operator surface `docs/operations.md` §Multi-writer co-writer mounts) adds the posture gauge `mount_posture` (`writer` | `reader` | `co-writer` — the one word that says which of the three shapes a daemon is) and the `cowriter` object: `mw_role`, `custody_endpoint`, `admissions` (1 per admitted mount and then flat), `admission_refusals` (every refusal names its RUNG — the five-rung ladder is declaration → bit 14 durable on EVERY volume → this node named a writer member of the claim set → a live authority lease → a device registrant under the standing WERO hold), `accounting_refusals` (ownership accounting a co-writer may not do — the specific claim, the W1 incarnation retire (a lifetime retire is durable ownership state and the §5.1 fence is process-local, so W1 stays authority-only BY DECISION: a co-writer's small overwrite rides CoW-rewrite + shipped free), the ownership recovery walk, direct device reclaim, and allocator-level frees no product path reaches; **allocation left this list with the lane grant and the router-level terminal free left it with the S9 co-writer FREE path** — a rewrite's displaced free now SHIPS as `PublishCall::FreeBlocks` (publish schema 3) and the AUTHORITY executes the whole ladder (`begin_free` → tier purge → reclaim enqueue → `finish_free`, the §6.8 item-3 grace ring and S7's quarantine composing inside), exactly-once under the `(lease_epoch, request_id)` dedup window (S8's window reused; retries never re-key across a re-join, and a fenced era's in-flight frees refuse by era), with the freed offset re-entering the free supply of the lane `b % W` names; the free-path rows ride the publish ledger — `meta_ship_publish.free_shipped_blocks` (the rewrite-engagement instrument: its delta must account for a co-writer row's displaced blocks) / `free_served_blocks` / `free_replays` (the witness engaging) / `free_stale_refusals` (the era fence) / `free_ship_failures` (**≈ 0**: each is a leak-safe durably-free offset unreturned until the authority's next derivation) / `free_refused_blocks` (the owner's `Refused` verdicts — a genuine double release, the class named on the owner's log line; `served + refused + non-terminal ≡ the peers' shipped`) — so `accounting_refusals` growth on a rewriting co-writer is a BUG again, not the honest append-only gap it used to be; contracts `tests/mw_cowriter_free_tests.rs`. **Finding 15's supply closure (2026-09-06, `.benchmarks/2026-09-06-cowriter-free-refcount-leak.md`, contracts `tests/mw_cowriter_free_leak_tests.rs`):** a recomputed publish's post-commit free set now includes the caller frame's **RAM-only lifetimes** (a block the frame both takes and releases that neither the head nor the composed map names — an epoch-fed overlay dest superseded by a write-through before any save persisted it; the head→composed diff cannot release what no map ever held, so it was freed by NOBODY), an own-mint map blob's lineage closes **exactly once** (`reclaimed_own_mints` memo: the save tail and the insert chokepoint's orphan reclaim were two issuers, and a stale clone re-inserted after a lock-free discard re-armed the free at every later discard — the s11 row's 3,449 refused frees, `publish_blob_orphan_reclaim_dedups`), a live laned co-writer's never-published mint returns to its OWN lane free list (`cowriter.unpublished_recycles`; the quiet abandon stays for poisoned eras and foreign lanes), and the served compose's displaced-blob free runs under the authority-accounting scope (a partial authority's own blob was shipping to the set authority)). **The recompute arm's local hygiene rides the served reply since publish schema 15 (2026-09-07, `.benchmarks/2026-09-07-cowriter-claim-anomaly-lineage.md`, contracts `tests/mw_cowriter_free_leak_tests.rs` §1c):** `PutDone`/`DeltaUsed`/`MapMigrated` carry the offsets the authority's ladder answered `Freed`, and `DataRouter::retire_recomputed_parked` retires exactly those parked rewrite-epoch keys AT THE REPLY under the `retire_shipped_free_tracking` discipline (`cowriter.recomputed_retires`; a key whose stamp is no longer the offset's live incarnation names a lifetime this mount already re-minted and is touched by nobody) — before it they waited for the epoch CLOSE, and the fpp fleet's grace → harvest → claim loop beat the close on ~1 % of every recompute-freed block (`block_claim_anomalies` 1,156–1,402 per phase Σ 8 co-writers, `free_shipped_blocks` flat). **The fleet's anomaly population turned out to be the authority's OWN publishes (2026-09-07 row 1D, `.benchmarks/2026-09-07-cowriter-claim-anomaly-population.md`, contracts `tests/mw_authority_recycled_binding_tests.rs` §4):** the S11 assembler's fold of a co-writer's shipped slices displaces the co-writer's block through the authority's local recompute — a free no served reply can name — so since publish schema 16 EVERY reply frame carries the authority's **lane-free notices** for the frame's client (`PublishReplyFrame::lane_frees`, queued BEFORE the ladder runs so the reply that hands the offset back through a harvest was built after the notice and carries it; `cowriter.lane_free_notices`), guarded by the harvest's `grant_seq` (a notice below an offset's grant tag names a lifetime the co-writer already re-minted and touches nothing, `lane_free_notices_reminted`) — the schema-15 parked-key retire stays correct and necessary and moved the fleet gauge by nothing — and `local_commit_refusals` (**should stay 0** — nonzero means a daemon surface still commits metadata directly instead of shipping it, i.e. S8's un-routed-daemon gap meeting a real workload). The D0 Layer-B2 gate is UNCHANGED: a co-writer enters through `KvMetaBackend::open_co_writer`, reachable only with an admission decision, so `writer_guard_mode` gained the `co-writer` row without weakening one refusal — and `BlockAllocator`'s gate became `plane_gate` with TWO classes (the reader's text and sites untouched) because a device offset's OWNERSHIP is metadata, which is exactly the authority a co-writer lacks while holding data authority under custody. The ALLOCATION arms then split off into `alloc_plane_gate`, whose co-writer class keys on the allocator's engaged LANE: with a granted lane they pass (placing a block in a residue class no peer mints in is a data act), without one they refuse verbatim as before — the relaxation is "an owned lane", never "a co-writer". **Under the ARMED symmetric plane (PR 8, 2026-09-15, dark) this partition is SUPERSEDED for the armed writer: `MAX_LANES` and the power-of-two stranding table do not apply — the writer allocates from RANGED BLOCK GRANTS its data volume's allocation-lease holder carves out of a durable per-volume bitmap (`src/block_grant.rs`, `src/data_alloc_bitmap.rs`, the paragraph above), and the unarmed S9 path stays byte-identical.**

**Read-path program families** (`docs/design-read-path.md` §Observability — semantics + regression thresholds there): the serve-latency decomposition campaign (2026-08-01, `.benchmarks/2026-08-01-serve-decomposition.md`) adds the ALWAYS-ON read residence families — `read_serve_phase_ns` (per-op: prelude/meta_resolve/key_resolve/classify_probe/sf_wait/block_fetch/binding_check/slice_out/post_validate/total), `read_fill_phase_ns` (per-fill: dev_queue/dev_service at the NvmeBlockDev funnel, fetch_dma/decode/admission/deposit/fill_total), and the fuse3-side `read_transport_phase_ns` (queue_wait/dispatch_lag/reply_commit/transport_total — `fio clat − transport_total` = the kernel-side residue by subtraction; all three bucket through the shared `#[path]` core `crates/squeezefs-ipc/src/latency_core.rs`, so the tables compose end-to-end) — plus `fuse3_read_inplace_replies` (the P2 in-place READ reply engagement gauge: the campaign found the arm structurally disengaged since `be82794` — the dispatch loop `take()`d the session connection — and rewired it; on an armed session this must account ≈ every READ); `singleflight_waiter_result_serves` (R1a cohort serves); `hot_block_{hits,misses,evictions,probation_drops,dehydrate_skips,current_bytes}` (R4 RAM tier); `read_fill_publishes_skipped` / `read_tier_admissions` / `read_tier_admission_ghost_hits` / `read_tier_admission_mode` (R1b second-touch admission — skipped ≈ streamed cold blocks); `prefetch_{issued,completed,wasted,inflight_bytes,window_hwm,foreground_waits,evicted_unconsumed,active_streams}` (R2 pipeline — `evicted_unconsumed` is the refetch-spiral detector); `ranged_{reads,read_bytes,read_unaligned_bounces,read_rebinds,read_ghost_escalations}` (R3 — `ranged_read_bytes` vs user bytes is the rand-4k amplification bound; `get_obj` counts ranged ops by design; `ghost_escalations` = hybrid-I/O second-touch → whole-block admissions); `read_lane_{fetches,fetch_bytes,holds,serves,serve_bytes,hold_retired,hold_evicted_unconsumed,wasted,depth_target,hold_bytes,inflight_bytes,armed}` (the read-lane campaign, 2026-08-01 — `src/read_lane.rs`, `.benchmarks/2026-08-01-read-lane.md`: the **hold** — a ledger-invisible, purge-integrated (R-6 fifth arm), coverage-retired completed-fill store — is the shipped default win on the fio-gap read plateau (+12.8 % qd8 / +14.5 % qd32 vs the `SQUEEZEFS_READ_LANE=0` A0 control, deep-qd cohort `read_amp` 1.41 → 1.11; `serves` is the cohort-stability instrument, `hold_evicted_unconsumed` the spiral detector, hold budget = the derived consume-behind window on R5 component `read_lane_hold`); the **ahead lane** (ledger-invisible whole-block fetches where R2 declines below its AIMD start window — `resident_share < 2` since 2026-08-05) is **probe-governed** (`read_lane_depth_probe_{ups,backoffs}` — the 2026-08-05 read-throughput campaign's engage-governor: the write-side `ProbeCore` reused verbatim; probes launch on saturated epochs with R5 headroom, adopt only when total fill delivery responds, retreat + cool down on dead gain, bleed to depth 0 on unsaturated epochs; `SQUEEZEFS_READ_LANE_DEPTH` pins for measurement, `0` = ahead off — the hold-only A/B control); both OPEN-LOOP depth derivations (BDP, AIMD window) were FALSIFIED by counted brackets on demand-concurrent venues (ahead-fetches died FIFO-unconsumed racing the demand cohort, −19 %) — the falsified venues are now the governor's duty-cycle-bounded RETREAT arm; the hold-churn campaign (2026-08-05 part 2 — the field's dead-gain acceptance row) adds `read_lane_covered_skips` (the marginal-issue walk: demand-front/store-covered blocks advance the lane cursor as sync probes, never a task or depth slot) and `read_lane_hold_ahead_evictions` (the ahead-class subset of `hold_evicted_unconsumed` — landing-zone pressure: movement within an epoch is zero probe headroom), and fixed the COVERAGE-CREDIT truth (dest-armed fetch-loop serves credited from the post-slice buffer — 0 for every sub-read past a block's first, killing coverage retirement on deep-qd cohort rows; dest arms now credit inline on the full block); classifier gained classified-membership tolerance ±64×len (run-building stays exact — the qd-reorder lane wedge fix); Red ⇒ 0 with `read_lane_inflight` converging by completion); the **READ copy ledger** (read-copy-count campaign, 2026-08-02, `.benchmarks/2026-08-02-read-copy-count.md` — the read twin of the write path's copy census): `read_copy_{dest,bounce}_bytes` / `read_{dest,fill}_dma_bytes` / `ipc_arena_copy_bytes` attribute every daemon CPU pass over read payload bytes at its site — closure (`dest + bounce + dest_dma ≈ kernel user bytes`; `dest + arena + dest_dma ≈ il bytes out`) is the row-validity instrument, `bounce` must stay ≈ 0 on cold il rows (E-IL1 zero-copy slices), `ipc_read_dest_serves ≡ ipc_async_handoffs` on il read rows (E-IL2 arena-dest serves; `SQUEEZEFS_IL_READ_DEST=0` is the A/B lever), and `nt_read_serve_bytes` is the `SQUEEZEFS_NT_READ_SERVE` lever's engagement (dest-arm serve copies ride NT stores — the counted +11 % EXA-cold-read win; floor `SQUEEZEFS_NT_READ_SERVE_MIN`, default 256 KiB, keeps rand-4k/warm-small serves cached); the **A1 warm-serve split** (third-party read-audit adjudication 2026-08-04, D12 board item 5) adds `read_copy_warm_serve_bytes` — the SUBSET of `read_copy_dest_bytes` sourced from warm tier buffers (the hot-block / read-lane-hold / NVMe read-cache serve arms; closure equations unchanged verbatim, cold dest residual = `dest − warm_serve`), and records the A1 decision rule: the tier-buffer handoff for warm serves gets BUILT only if a counted warm row's `warm_serve` share prices it in (never the auditor's +15–25 % blanket); **R-4 read-zc-serve (e2e perf audit read board #4/#5, 2026-09-05, `.benchmarks/2026-09-05-r4-read-zc-serve.md`) split that bucket PER ARM** — `read_copy_{hot,hold,cache}_serve_bytes` partition `warm_serve` exactly (counted at the copy, the handler's dest arms AND the R-2 fast probe's window copy — which had been OUTSIDE the ledger since R-2: a warm armed row could not close) and `read_copy_fill_slice_bytes` names the cold whole-block fill → dest slice-out (the 2.69 row's one daemon pass, ⊆ `dest − warm`) — and adjudicated the lever: on a zc-armed session the kernel reply primitive is `READ_FIXED(fd → slot)`, fd-SOURCED (device fd on the direct leg, the per-ent bounce memfd otherwise) and never VA-sourced, so the warm/cold-slice passes were the daemon's tier → bounce copy plus the kernel's bridge — a DAEMON prerequisite (an fd-addressable tier buffer), not a kernel one. **`SQUEEZEFS_READ_ZC_SERVE`** (default ON since the 2026-09-05 field bracket — daemon CPU/GiB −24 % cold / −25 % warm on the unaligned population, A-B-B-A both orders, closure exact; `0` is the A/B control) builds the whole-block READ fill pool as ONE `MAP_SHARED` memfd slab (`AlignedBufPool::new_memfd_slabbed`, a SEPARATE pool from the write path's `ALIGNED_BUF_POOL`) so the router's hot/hold/fill-slice arms and the fast probe (`PayloadSink::offer_fd_body`) hand the transport the tier buffer's own `(fd, offset)` (`ReplyData::zc_fd_body` / `FastReadProbe::ServedFd` → `CommitMsg::body_fd`) and the queue worker bridges it into the folios with the body `Bytes` as the keepalive until the CQE (the buffer-lease law by construction; a failed fd-source bridge re-stages through the bounce — `PendDone::Restage`, never a lost reply): 2.0 → 1.0 passes/byte warm, 2.69 → 1.69 cold-slice, no kernel change; engagement `read_zc_pool_serve_bytes` (a closure term) + `read_zc_pool_serve_warm_bytes`, `fuse3_zc_fd_body_replies`, tripwire `fuse3_zc_fd_body_fallbacks` ≈ 0; the **READ dest-window lease** (copy-elimination phase 1, 2026-08-06, `.benchmarks/2026-08-06-read-dest-lease.md` — the CPU-wall ruling's first mechanism; kernel adjudication: the fuse-over-uring COMMIT consumes only `commit_id`/`qid` and copies the reply from the REGISTER-time ent VA / kernel-minted kmbuf, so a tier-buffer lease can only RELOCATE the serve copy — the inverse lease ships instead) adds `read_dest_lease_bytes`: cold 4 KiB-aligned sub-block kernel windows on passthrough volumes DMA device bytes STRAIGHT into the reply's registered dest (the ranged primitive, lease-tagged; the serve dest-copy deleted — local A-B-B-A +28.6 % GB/s, daemon CPU/byte −50 %, engagement exact), a SUBSET of `read_dest_dma_bytes` (closure unchanged); dest-leaseable traffic stands the R2/lane speculative fills down (the 2 s yield stamp) and is device-true by design; `SQUEEZEFS_READ_DEST_LEASE=0` is the A/B control (pre-campaign shape byte-identical); contracts `tests/read_dest_lease_tests.rs`; `read_admission_{evicted_unhit,wasted_bytes,governor_denials,governor_clamped}` (scan-resistant admission governor, 2026-07-26 — the beyond-budget random-read collapse: `evicted_unhit` is the `prefetch_evicted_unconsumed` sibling for admissions, `wasted_bytes`÷device-read-bytes ≤ ~`SQUEEZEFS_READ_ADMISSION_FILL_PCT` % (default 5) is the bounded-waste verdict under churn, `governor_denials` must stay ≈ 0 on fitting working sets; evidence `.benchmarks/2026-07-26-read-admission-scan-resistance.md`; `read_admission_stream_transients` — the transient stream window, 2026-07-29: classified-stream ghost-hit re-fills held transient (probation, no publish, ledger-invisible) by the same governor clamp+tokens, refusals deliberately OFF the `governor_denials` instrument, stream-granted victims full-shortfall in `wasted_bytes` but exempt from `evicted_unhit` — sustained growth on beyond-budget stream loops is the designed steady state); `read_odirect_{tier_serves,ghost_admits}` + `read_device_true_reads` + `direct_device_true` (hybrid I/O, 2026-07-15 user directive: O_DIRECT serves/admits like buffered by default — the mount-scoped `-o direct_device_true` / `SQUEEZEFS_DIRECT_DEVICE_TRUE=1` escape restores strict device-true O_DIRECT for `.benchmarks` amplification measurement); `mem_budget_{bytes,pressure_bytes,gauge_sum_bytes,unreclaimable_bytes,level,yellow_events,red_events,hard_backstops,backstop_active,tier_publish_paused,floors_clamped,dehydrate_paused,components{…}}` + `read_tier_publishes_paused` + `parked_gate_{waits,self_flushes,timeouts}` (R5 authority — red_events with no OOM is the designed outcome under pressure; `hard_backstops`/`parked_gate_timeouts` growing on quiet workloads means a convergence regression, §5.7 Red semantics); the rebind-starvation fix (2026-08-04 field capture — the fio read phase racing the write phase's writeback/fold promotion backlog EIO'd hundreds of pure reads through the ladder's "did not settle after 24 binding rebinds" exit) adds `stale_binding_escalations` — read-path rebind-ladder exhaustions that escalated to the SERIALIZED settle arm (one device-direct resolve-then-fetch under the block's `BLOCK_FLUSH_LOCKS` (3) + the ino's `INODE_META_LOCKS` (3.5), the write path's own extended order — an arm legal writer churn cannot beat) instead of surfacing EIO: growth = sustained displacement churn outrunning the latch-free ladder (a latency signal, pair with `stale_binding_rebinds`), and a "did not settle" EIO now means a genuinely broken binding only — a fetch/decode failure on a CURRENT binding (the propagate arm), a write-side (non-escalating) exhaustion, or a mutator outside the stripe/merge disciplines (which also trips `invariant_tripwires` via `read_settle_lost_serialized`). Contracts: `tests/rebind_starvation_tests.rs`. Finding 51 (2026-09-07, `.benchmarks/2026-09-07-read-settle-lost-serialized-authority.md`) adds `served_binding_witnesses` — incarnation words an AUTHORITY published because a SERVED layout publish adopted a FOREIGN-lane block (the serve is its only witness of the co-writer's completed DMA behind a key it can never mint; its own `begin_free` of the offset's previous lifetime had retired the word, so before the witness every authority read/fold of a RECYCLED co-writer block failed validation for ever — the s11-mpiio row's 326 `read_settle_lost_serialized` tripwires and 101 fsync EIOs); 0 by construction on solo and co-writer mounts, ≈ the co-writers' inserted blocks on a rewrite row, and `read_settle_lost_serialized` keeps its must-stay-0 meaning for the remaining class (a retire/claim of a currently-bound key outside the disciplines). Its phase-B1 addendum (§8 of the same note): a SERVED layout publish that displaces an index the authority holds an OPEN device-overlay record on (the assembler's shipped-slice shape) is a foreign durable `Merge` design-overlay-overwrite §5.7's one-authority screen never saw — the served commit lands on the backend directly — so the recompute freed the record's captured old binding and its settle retried a dead key for ever (`STALE BLOCK-KEY BINDING refused` 28,039× in one B1 phase, all lane-0 offsets, the write-arm settle at 50 ms per wedged record); the served-publish screen (`publish::install_served_displacement_sink`, beside the rung-18 invalidation) now supersedes such records at the commit — counted on `overlay_superseded_by_served_publish`, a legal peer so never the `overlay_foreign_merge` tripwire — and the settle's dead-capture belt supersedes any record whose captured old binding names a dead lifetime (`overlay_superseded_dead_old_binding`, 0 while the screen catches every displacement; its one refusal is the detection).

**Executor-primitive faces (e2e audit R-1, 2026-09-02, `.benchmarks/2026-09-02-r1-device-read-executor.md`):** the two `sqz_time` registries — squeezefs-ipc's and the fuse3 fork's `#[path]`-shared copy, each its own global lock + heap + `sqz-timer` thread — export `timer_{arms,tombstones_skipped,heap_entries,live_sleeps}` and `transport_timer_{…}` respectively (arms = every `sleep`/`timeout`/ticked-park registration; a tombstone = a cancelled arm the service thread later pops under the lock; `heap − live` ≈ tombstones queued). On the sqz kernel the kern READ hot path arms the TRANSPORT face at ≈ 0.35–0.45 per op (`InboundQueue::pop`'s ticked recv) and the daemon face at ≈ 0 — `nvme_dev.rs`'s per-`read_block` timeout engages only on the stock-kernel (`SQUEEZEFS_FUSE_ZC=0`), transform, unaligned and device-true postures; measured cost ≤ 3 % daemon CPU / < 0.2 % of clat, adjudicated not-fat (the contention knee sits at ≈ 1.8–2.2 M kernel IOPS). `daemon_cpu_ns_by_class` gained `fuse3-ur` (the FUSE-over-io_uring queue workers — half of a kern rand-4k row's CPU, formerly inside `other`) and `sqz-timer` (both timer threads). Contracts: `tests/audit_instruments_tests.rs`.

**Read board #3 faces (e2e audit R-3, 2026-09-03, `.benchmarks/2026-09-03-r3-fill-issue-economy.md`):** `zc_bridge_phase_ns` — the zc direct leg's four hops around one DMA, exact-sum, always-on, fetch leg only: `msg_hop` (handler send → queue worker take), `sq_wait` (take → the carrying `io_uring_enter`), `device_cq` (enter → CQE popped), `wake_hop` (popped → handler resumed), `total` (≡ Σ hops to the ns; `read_serve_phase_ns.block_fetch − total` = the handler's pre-send probes); op-trace stages `bridge_sent`/`bridge_taken` beside `dev_submit`/`dev_complete` on this leg. The attribution it produced — three cross-thread wakes at 35–47 µs each around a 40 µs device (`msg_hop` 28 / `device_cq` − device ≈ 60 / `wake_hop` 36 on the shipped R-2 shape, per op) — is why the READ dispatch law has a FUSED arm: **THE READ DISPATCH LAW (R-2 ⊕ R-3, `crates/fuse3/src/raw/connection/fast_dispatch.rs`)** — at the delivery CQE a fetched READ is (a) served inline if warm (R-2's try-only probe), else (b) fused onto the queue worker's own lane if the session is zc-armed and the size is at or under the fusion ceiling (`SQUEEZEFS_FUSE_ZC_READ_FUSION`), else (c) handed to the `fuse3-tpc` lane homed on the queue's CPU (R-2); `transport_fast_dispatch_serves + fuse3_zc_read_fusions + transport_fast_dispatch_demotes ≡ READs` is the pinned partition and the reap thread never blocks on any arm. **The fusion lever ships OFF on measurement**: alone against the pre-R-2 dispatch it won the field kern rand-4k row (+15.5 %), but composed behind R-2 it read −6.5 % vs R-2 alone (481 k vs 514 k; p50 +70 µs, p99 −66 %) — both levers converge on the reap thread's per-op serialization (the fused pass's run-queue waits replace the lane hop + the bridge's two worker hops at a net loss) and do not compound; the composed default binary is par with R-2 (507–510 k vs 503–507 k). `1` = the tail-shaped posture. The NvmeBlockDev lane worker now parks with its request-arrival wake IN the ring (an eventfd `READ` SQE the enqueue side writes through the L3 coalescer — a fill's issue never waits for an unrelated fill's completion): `dev_enters` / `dev_fills` (enters ÷ fills ≪ 1 under concurrency), `dev_wake_batches`, `dev_wake_writes` / `dev_wakes_elided`; test seam `nvme_dev::set_test_read_slow` (a linked `TIMEOUT` makes the DEVICE completion late while the worker stays free). The remaining kern term is the fused pass's run-queue wait (`read_transport_phase_ns.queue_wait` + `wake_hop` ≈ 184 of 247 µs daemon-visible); the il reap term (`ipc_direct_phase_ns.device_cq` − device ≈ 42–44 µs, `ipc_direct_inline_reaps` ≈ 8 % of direct ops) is named, not landed.

**Read board R-4 faces (e2e audit R-4, 2026-09-03, `.benchmarks/2026-09-03-r4-reap-thread-economy.md`):** the reap worker's per-op ledger (perf on `f3-ur*` at the field: 17.7 µs/op, 63 % kernel — the COMMIT_AND_FETCH's own work incl. 1.9 µs of `fuse_request_end`/`fuse_uring_req_end` lock contention, the fetch's nvme-tcp submit 2.5, two lane wakes 1.7; 37 % daemon — the pass loop 2.1, the R-2 probe ladder 1.8, instruments 1.0) convicted the daemon's per-op contended words, and the **worker CPU diet** ships: every per-op worker counter is a per-thread `ShardedCounter` (`crates/fuse3/src/raw/read_phase.rs`), the pool pend gauge takes ONE net-delta RMW per pass, the bridge-deadline scan runs on a deadline-derived period (`timeout/1024`), the eventfd drain is one `read(2)`, the pass-bottom completion list is reused — field kern rand-4k +0.7…+2.4 % IOPS (four brackets, both orders; sustained +2.4 %/+0.7 %), CPU/op −3…−4 %, the worker's pass work (`transport_reap_gap_ns.blind`) 6.2 → 4.7 µs. **The spin-before-park governor ships OFF** (`SQUEEZEFS_FUSE_IO_URING_SPIN_US`, default `0`; any value engages it with that cap — the window is 2 × the worker's own park-gap EWMA, only while its queues hold ops in flight, refused past the box's 80 % busy knee; rings carry `IORING_SETUP_TASKRUN_FLAG` so a spin sees deferred task work without entering): at the field it deleted 69 % of the worker's parks (0.76 → 0.24/op) and moved no latency term — `msg_hop` / `device_cq` / `wake_hop` unchanged to the µs — at +5.5 % CPU/op, so the worker's ingress terms are its KERNEL residence per enter and its run-queue standing on a 75–80 %-busy box, not its park/wake. Ledger `transport_spin_{absorbed,expired,ns,refused_busy,window_us}` (`absorbed + expired ≡` spins; 0 on the default). The shared law lives in `crates/squeezefs-ipc/src/spin_governor_core.rs` (`#[path]`-included by the root crate as `spin_governor` and by the fork). **The ledger's KERNEL term shipped as a kernel patch (2026-09-06, `docs/design-kernel-bg-per-queue.md`, `.benchmarks/2026-09-06-kernel-bg-per-queue-ab.md`):** the `fuse_request_end`/`fuse_uring_req_end` contention was `fc->bg_lock`, taken twice per uring completion; sqz series patch **0031** (6.19.14 + 7.1 tracks; **0026** on 7.2) gives every uring queue its own background budget (its share of `max_background` = exactly its ent count on this daemon's INIT reply, so the semantics are unchanged and only the locking moves — §2.5) — the A A B B on squeeze-test (same 1.2.1 `dist` daemon) read the worker **17.4 → 14.3 µs/op (−18 %)**, `native_queued_spin_lock_slowpath` **12.8 % → 0.11 %** of its cycles, kern rand-4k **+9 % IOPS** (508 → 556k sustained), p99 −13…−17 %, seq and the il control par, no WARN/lockdep. Field rows after 2026-09-06 19:48 UTC run 0031; comparisons against earlier field rows must say so.

**Read handler economy (e2e audit R-5, 2026-09-08, `.benchmarks/2026-09-08-r5-read-handler-economy.md`):** the kernel READ handler + router single-block serve are OFF the allocator and off every shared cache line per op — the warm ladder serve went 8 → 0 allocs/op and the cold FUSE-zc leg 11 → 1 (the injected fetch's box; the live `ZcReadServe::for_connection` arm borrows the handler's connection and boxes nothing), pinned by `tests/kernel_op_economy_tests.rs` (budgets warm 1 / cold zc 2) — via inline custody epochs (`CustodyEpochs`), the by-reference single-block resolve (`block_key_in` on the serve arm; `load_striped_block_keys` only for the partial/anomalous shapes), the stack-key W2 gate, a borrowed fetch-ladder key with an in-place recheck, the retired `backing` Arc (the router's read primitive returns `Bytes` alone — the Arc kept a transmuted slice alive that E-IL1 retired), and the borrowed key resolvers `clean_block_key_ref` / `BlockKeyRef` / `with_allocator_for_key` (R #7: the cold leg's four re-parses were 7 allocs + 6 refcount RMWs per op); `read_serve_phase_ns` / `read_fill_phase_ns` record on per-thread stripes (`ShardedLatencyHistogram`, exact fold, JSON byte-identical) and the twenty per-op READ counters are `ShardedAtomic`; phase boundaries chain span-form (13 → 9 clock reads per warm serve; the zc leg now records `classify_probe`); kvmap partial windows fill ONE arena (R #8) and the R2 residency check clones no `Bytes` (R #9). No knob. The R-1 residual is moot for READ (since R-2 a kernel READ never traverses `InboundQueue::pop`). Dev-box microbench: fp build −28 %, cold key resolves −41…−57 %, 8-thread phase record −72 %; **the field `rr_4k` CPU/op row on squeeze-test is owed.**

## Branch & Commit Workflow

The integration branch is **`dev`** (not `main`; `main` is reserved for releases). `origin/HEAD` points at `origin/dev`.

- Branch from `dev`: `feat/`, `fix/`, `refactor/`, `perf/`, `docs/` prefixes. Never commit directly to `dev`.
- Tests-first cycle: write failing tests → implement → refine → commit each logical step separately.
- Merge with `--ff-only`; rebase feature branches if `dev` has diverged. Delete branches after merge.
- Conventional commits: `type(scope): description`. Messages explain WHY.
- **Versioning = release train + git commit** (`docs/operations.md` §Versioning & releases; user directive 2026-07-24 superseding the commit-only posture): package versions track the **release train** (currently 1.2.4) and are bumped **as a release act** across the root and the first-party crates (`crates/fuse3`, `crates/squeezefs-ipc`, `crates/squeezefs-preload`) together — never per commit, never by CI. Git commits remain the fine-grained identity (`--version` shows both); release tags (`stable-*`/`lts-*`, created manually as a release act) remain the only release names.

See the full TDD Development Workflow section above for the detailed phased process.

## Authoritative Supporting Documents

While this file is the combined one source of truth for agents and contributors:

- `README.md` / `QUICKSTART.md` — user-facing CLI, NVMe-oF, and bare-metal setup.
- `docs/reference-clients-survey.md` — the DAOS-client + JuiceFS reference fast-FUSE client survey (see **Reference fast-FUSE clients** above).
- `docs/design-preload-interception.md` — the L4 LD_PRELOAD POSIX-interception data path (`libsqueezefs-il`): charter labeling discipline, IPC protocol/gates, the survey's P3-A adoption item; since v1.1 also the **libaio interposers** (`io_setup`/`io_submit`/`io_getevents` lane-split — libaio drivers are valid, engagement-verified il instruments; Rev 4 + `.benchmarks/2026-07-19-v1.1-libaio-interposers.md`); since 2026-08-04 (Rev 17) also **direct-link (DT_NEEDED) support** — `-lsqueezefs_il` is a supported linkage (SONAME + `-z nodelete`, linked-mode detection line, gate legs 1e/2b-linked; same KD-7 pairing — operations.md carries the link-order + setuid/env-scrubbing story).
- `docs/design-sdk.md` — the native SDK program (`squeezefs-sdk` + the `libsqueezefs` C ABI): arena-native buffers under the buffer-lease law (deletes the client-side S1 write copy + read consume copy — `.benchmarks/2026-08-02-read-copy-count.md` is the pricing basis), io_uring-shaped batch API, the SKD-4 versioned compat window (SDK sessions only; the shim keeps same-commit KD-7), per-open durability classes composing with design-rewrite-program §9.3. Design phase 2026-08-04; implementation is the follow-on `feat/sdk-…` ladder.
- `docs/PROFILING_AND_GATES.md` (historical) — content now consolidated here.
